Method: 采用基于图像的自监督对比学习作为适配策略，利用内部心脏MR数据集进行模型微调，并通过消融实验研究批次大小和数据集规模对性能的影响。

Result: 适配后的ViT模型在四种常见心脏MR序列分类任务上AUC超过0.75，优于传统监督训练方法，且在BraTS和ADNI等外部MR数据集上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 自监督对比学习是ViT模型有效适配医学影像领域的可行策略，能够克服预训练特征的领域迁移障碍，为ViT在医学影像中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) models, utilizing self-attention mechanisms, have demonstrated robust generalization capabilities across various vision tasks, including image classification. However, these models, typically pretrained on general public datasets, often lack the specialized domain knowledge necessary for medical imaging applications. In this study, we investigate the adaptation of ViT models, specifically for cardiac magnetic resonance (MR) images, using an in-house dataset. We found that pretrained ViT features do not effectively transfer to the cardiac MR domain. To overcome this limitation, we introduce an adaptation strategy that utilizes image-based self-supervised contrastive learning, demonstrating superior performance compared to traditional supervised training approaches. Moreover, our adapted ViT model exhibits strong generalization to external MR datasets such as BraTS and ADNI. Through ablation studies, we further investigate the impact of batch size and dataset scale on performance. Ultimately, our adapted model achieves classification AUC exceeding 0.75 across the four most common cardiac MR sequences.

</details>


### [75] [Parameter-Efficient VLMs for Gastrointestinal Endoscopy: Medical Image Generation and Clinical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24792)
*Ojonugwa Oluwafemi Ejiga Peter,Frederick Akor Ejiga,Fahmi Khalifa,Md Mahmudur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双管道PEFT模型，结合Florence-2视觉语言模型进行医学VQA，并利用LoRA与Stable Diffusion 2.1生成高质量、保护隐私的GI内窥镜合成图像。在Kvasir-VQA数据集上，VQA模型ROUGE-1达0.92，LoRA合成计算成本降低约90%，FBD达1450，性能优于FLUX、MSDM等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GI内窥镜AI系统受限于标注数据不足、严格的隐私政策以及传统模型微调的高成本瓶颈，阻碍了AI模型在临床诊断中的可靠应用与规模化落地。

Method: 采用双管道PEFT框架：(1) 使用Florence-2视觉语言模型通过PEFT进行医学VQA微调，降低计算成本并提升可解释性；(2) 使用LoRA结合Stable Diffusion 2.1生成高质量GI合成图像，避免患者隐私泄露。实验基于Kvasir-VQA数据集。

Result: VQA模型ROUGE-1为0.92，ROUGE-L为0.91，BLEU从0.08提升至0.24。rank-4 LoRA合成最优：保真度0.290，一致性0.730，FBD为1450，计算成本降低约90%。私有数据集微调效果优于公开数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了GI内窥镜中AI的临床潜力，在FBD和语义对齐方面优于FLUX、MSDM、Kandinsky 2.2等模型，为临床AI中的VQA和合成数据生成提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: The major limitations of gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy AI systems arise from a shortage of annotated data, strict privacy policies, and significant bottlenecks in conventional model fine-tuning. Such limitations impede the successful application of sophisticated AI models in clinical practice, particularly affecting the reliability and scalability of diagnosis. In this paper, we present a dual-pipeline PEFT model that addresses two fundamental problems: medical Visual Question Answering (VQA) and the generation of privacy-preserving synthetic data. For clinical VQA, we adopt the Florence-2 vision-language model. Leveraging PEFT enhances model interpretability while substantially reducing the computational cost of training. Simultaneously, we employ Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with Stable Diffusion 2.1 to generate high-quality GI images that enhance training databases without violating patient privacy. This research utilized the Kvasir-VQA dataset. Our Florence-2 VQA model achieved ROUGE-1 of 0.92, ROUGE-L of 0.91, and BLEU score improvements from 0.08 to 0.24. Fine-tuning on private datasets consistently showed better results than fine-tuning on public datasets. The rank-4 LoRA synthesis achieved optimal performance with a fidelity score of 0.290, an agreement score of 0.730, and a Frechet BiomedCLIP Distance (FBD) of 1450, reducing computational costs by almost 90 percent. This framework improves the clinical potential of AI in GI endoscopy. Compared to FLUX, MSDM, and Kandinsky 2.2, our model demonstrates superior FBD and strong semantic alignment. While other models lead in Fidelity or Agreement, our lower FBD indicates better image-text coherence. These results establish our approach as a robust solution for enhancing VQA and synthetic data generation in clinical AI.

</details>


### [76] [DUEL: Adversarial Self-Play for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24794)
*Lin Qiu,Hanqing Zeng,Yao Liu,Bingjun Sun,Guangdeng Liao,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DUEL是一个自进化的后训练框架，通过两个策略（Challenger和Solver）的对抗交互生成监督信号，无需人工标注即可提升VLM的视觉推理能力。Challenger生成真实陈述和硬负样本，Solver验证两者与图像的匹配程度，并引入长度归一化的对数似然奖励来稳定优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL优化依赖昂贵的高质量标注难以扩展，无监督方法因弱视觉基础和缺乏可靠验证信号可能产生偏差。

Method: 从同一预训练VLM初始化两个策略，Challenger生成基于图像的真实陈述及其最小扰动的硬负样本，Solver验证两个陈述与图像的匹配度；采用长度归一化的对数似然奖励替代二元结果监督。

Result: DUEL在无需人工标注、外部奖励模型或图像编辑工具的情况下，持续提升了视觉推理和鲁棒判别能力。

Conclusion: 自我对抗的监督信号能够在无外部标注下有效增强VLM的细粒度视觉辨别能力和推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning capability of vision-language models (VLMs). However, RL-based optimization typically depends on costly high-quality annotations that are difficult to scale. Existing unsupervised alternatives may drift toward biased solutions due to weak visual grounding and the lack of reliable verification signals. We propose a self-evolving post-training framework, DUEL, where supervision emerges from adversarial interactions between two policies initialized from the same pretrained VLM. A Challenger generates an image-grounded true claim together with a minimally perturbed hard-negative counterpart, while a Solver verifies both claims against the image, encouraging fine-grained visual discrimination under near-neighbor semantics. To stabilize optimization, we introduce a length-normalized log-likelihood reward that preserves informative optimization signals beyond binary outcome supervision and improves learning stability under sparse feedback. Experiments show that DUEL consistently improves visual reasoning and robust discrimination without additional human annotations, external reward models, or image editing tools.

</details>


### [77] [HCL-FF: Hierarchical and Contrastive Learning for Forward-Forward Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24797)
*Jie-En Yao,Hong-En Chen,C. -C. Jay Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HCL-FF框架，通过层级对比学习解决Forward-Forward算法中缺乏层级协调和特征语义模糊的问题，在三个数据集上达到FF方法新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FF算法虽然避免了反向传播的生物学不可塑性问题，但其纯局部优化缺乏跨层层次协调，且goodness解耦后的特征表示不受约束、语义模糊。

Method: (1) 粗到细的层次学习策略，引导表示从低级特征到高级语义； (2) 监督对比学习目标，在goodness解耦后强制类别判别性对齐。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet上分别提升+5.46%、+17.00%、+12.51%，达到FF方法新SOTA。

Conclusion: 层次化与对比学习有效弥补了FF算法在层级协调和特征判别性上的不足，显著提升性能。

Abstract: Deep neural networks trained with backpropagation have achieved outstanding performance in vision tasks but remain biologically implausible, computationally demanding, and difficult to interpret. The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm offers a promising alternative by training each layer independently through local goodness objectives. However, its purely local optimization lacks hierarchical coordination across layers, and the decoupling of goodness from features leaves the representations unconstrained and semantically ambiguous. We propose a Hierarchical and Contrastive Learning FF framework (HCL-FF) to address these limitations. HCL-FF introduces (1) a coarse-to-fine hierarchical learning strategy that guides representations from low-level cues to high-level semantics, and (2) a supervised contrastive objective that enforces class-discriminative alignment after goodness decoupling. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet demonstrate that HCL-FF achieves new state-of-the-art performance among FF-based methods, with notable accuracy gains of +5.46%, +17.00%, and +12.51%, respectively.

</details>


### [78] [Divide-and-Conquer Inference for Large-Scale Visual Recognition with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24799)
*Zhipeng Ye,Jiaqi Huang,Feng Jiang,Qiufeng Wang,Yikang Duan,Dawei Wang,Xihang Zhou,Qian Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出分治推理（DCI）策略，通过递归分解大规模图像分类任务并引入动态剪枝机制，有效缓解多模态大语言模型在长序列识别中的性能崩塌问题，无需额外训练即可显著提升分类精度和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在标签空间扩展时出现性能崩塌（Performance Collapse in Long Sequence Recognition），信息论分析表明根源在于信息熵增长与注意力稀释/衰减之间的根本性冲突，导致模型在处理超长提示时信噪比不足。

Method: 提出Divide-and-Conquer Inference (DCI) 测试时缩放策略，递归将全局分类任务分解为多个局部子问题，并采用动态剪枝机制压缩搜索空间，从而改善局部信噪比并缓解长序列推理中的权重稀释问题。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-21K等基准测试上，DCI一致提升分类精度；轻量级开源模型可媲美甚至超越前沿闭源大模型；同时相比传统自注意力的二次复杂度，DCI实现了更优的缩放行为和推理加速。

Conclusion: DCI是一种模型无关、即插即用的范式，为大语言模型在大规模场景下的推理精度缩放提供了高效解决方案，无需任何额外训练或微调。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across a wide range of vision language tasks. However, when applied to large scale image classification, their performance degrades significantly as the label space expands a phenomenon we define as Performance Collapse in Long Sequence Recognition. Through an information theoretic analysis, we reveal that this collapse stems from a fundamental conflict between the escalating information entropy and the prominent attention dilution and decay within attention mechanisms, which impairs the model's ability to maintain a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio when processing extremely long prompts. To mitigate this, we propose Divide-and-Conquer Inference (DCI), a novel test-time scaling strategy for visual recognition with MLLMs. DCI recursively decomposes complex global classification tasks into multiple simpler, localized subproblems and employs a dynamic pruning mechanism to compress the search space. This method effectively improves the local signal to noise ratio and model accuracy by mitigating the inherent weight dilution issues in long-sequence inference. Moreover, while traditional self-attention incurs a prohibitive quadratic computational complexity, DCI achieves more favorable scaling behavior and substantially accelerates inference in large scale classification scenarios. Extensive experiments on benchmarks such as ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-21K demonstrate that DCI consistently improves classification accuracy. This enables lightweight open-source models to rival or even surpass frontier closed-source giants without any additional training or fine-tuning. As a model-agnostic, plug-and-play paradigm, DCI offers an efficient approach for scaling the inferential precision of MLLMs in large-scale scenarios.

</details>


### [79] [Fishbone: From One 3D Asset to a Million Controllable Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24805)
*Yumeng He,Xiaoying Wang,Peihao Li,Yanjia Huang,Joe Masterjohn,Jiajun Wu,Leonidas Guibas,Yin Yang,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fishbone提出了一种基于肋-脊柱（rib-spine）表示的通用形状变形框架，通过提取网格的测地标量场、等高线肋和几何感知脊柱，实现对3D网格的实时、可控参数化变形，并支持简化空间动力学和动画。配合Fishbone-136K数据集，该框架在可控3D生成、机器人学习数据增强、交互式网格编辑和智能体生成等任务中展现出高效性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模可控3D资产对计算机图形学、具身AI、机器人学和交互式内容创作至关重要，但手动建模和绑定成本高昂，现有数据驱动方法依赖稀疏用户输入，参数化编辑框架需要手动设计的控制结构和特定类别的配置，缺乏统一且通用的形状变形表示。

Method: 受自然界脊柱和肋骨结构的启发，提出统一的肋-脊柱（rib-spine）表示：首先通过自适应热方法计算网格的测地标量场，提取等高线作为横截面肋（rib）；通过肋中心构建平滑的几何感知脊柱（spine）；利用高斯加权蒙皮将表面顶点与邻近的肋和脊柱结构关联。肋骨控制局部轮廓（厚度、方向、横截面变化），脊柱控制全局变形（弯曲、扭转、拉伸），同时支持简化空间仿真和关键帧动画。

Result: 构建了Fishbone-136K数据集（对Hunyuan3D进行肋-脊柱结构增强），并在可控3D生成、基于变形的机器人学习数据增强、交互式网格编辑和智能体生成等应用中验证了框架的有效性、高效性和通用性。

Conclusion: Fishbone框架通过统一的肋-脊柱表示实现了通用形状的可控参数化变形，兼顾局部与全局控制，支持实时交互和多种下游应用，为3D资产创建和编辑提供了一种灵活、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale controllable 3D assets are critical for computer graphics, embodied AI, robotics, and interactive content creation, yet creating diverse 3D assets remains challenging due to the high cost of manual modeling and rigging. Shape deformation offers a natural way to generate variations from existing meshes, but existing data-driven methods often rely on sparse user inputs, while parametric editing frameworks require manually designed control structures and category-specific configurations. Inspired by natural creatures, where a central spine governs global shape and cross-sectional ribs control local variation, we introduce Fishbone, a unified rib-spine representation for general shapes that supports controllable parametric mesh deformation, reduced-space dynamics, and animation. Given an input mesh, Fishbone computes a geodesic scalar field with an adaptive heat method, extracts iso-contours as cross-sectional ribs, constructs a smooth geometry-aware spine through rib centers, and associates surface vertices with nearby rib and spine structures using Gaussian-weighted skinning. The resulting representation enables real-time and predictable deformation: ribs control local profiles such as thickness, orientation, and cross-sectional variation, while the spine controls global bending, twisting, and stretching. The same structure also supports reduced-space simulation and keyframe animation. We further construct Fishbone-136K by augmenting Hunyuan3D with rib-spine structures, and demonstrate applications in controllable 3D generation, deformation-based data augmentation for robot learning, interactive mesh editing, and agentic generation. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and versatility of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [80] [CLIP-Guided SAM: Parameter-Efficient Semantic Conditioning for Promptable Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24807)
*Shayan Jalilian,Abdul Bais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIP-Guided SAM，通过轻量级多模态语义适配器将CLIP的文本、视觉和相似度特征直接注入SAM图像编码器，实现内部语义条件化，在低标注数据设置下实现高效分割，支持手动和半自动两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM虽然能生成高质量掩码，但缺乏语义理解能力，需要外部提示指定类别。现有方法（视觉-语言+SAM）通过外部提示耦合分阶段处理，效率低下且不够紧凑。

Method: 提出CLIP-Guided SAM框架，设计轻量级多模态语义适配器，将CLIP的文本、视觉和相似度特征注入SAM图像编码器内部，实现内部语义条件化。支持手动模式（文本+空间提示）和半自动文本模式（仅文本输入）。

Result: 在通用基准和专用下游任务上，相比SAM+PEFT基准、视觉-语言+SAM流水线、SAM 3及强半监督方法，CLIP-Guided SAM在参数高效的前提下达到更优或竞争力相当的性能。训练-测试提示一致性是关键设计原则。

Conclusion: CLIP-Guided SAM通过内部语义条件化有效解决了SAM语义盲点问题，在低标注数据场景下参数高效且性能优越，为可提示基础模型的语义增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Promptable foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) produce high-quality masks but remain semantically blind, relying on external prompts to specify categories. Existing vision-language approaches address this limitation by using external prompt coupling, where a vision-language model generates spatial prompts for SAM as a separate stage.
  We propose CLIP-Guided SAM, a parameter-efficient segmentation framework built on internal semantic conditioning. Instead of using semantic signals only to generate prompts, we inject CLIP-derived text, vision, and similarity features directly into SAM's image encoder through lightweight multi-modal semantic adapters. These adapters condition SAM's internal feature representations, allowing semantic information to influence mask prediction while preserving SAM's original promptable interface.
  Our framework is designed for low labeled-data settings and applies to both general-domain benchmarks and specialized downstream tasks. It supports two operating modes: Manual mode, for interactive segmentation with both text and spatial prompts, and Semi-Automatic text-only mode, for applications that require concept-specific segmentation using only textual input. We show that robustness depends on aligning training with the type of prompts used at inference, making train-test prompt consistency an important design principle.
  Through extensive experiments and ablations, we evaluate our method against SAM+PEFT baselines without semantic conditioning, vision-language + SAM pipelines, SAM 3, and strong semi-supervised segmentation methods that rely on large amounts of unlabeled data. Across these settings, CLIP-Guided SAM consistently achieves superior or competitive performance while remaining parameter-efficient in both training and deployment.

</details>


### [81] [Multiscale Real-Time Object Detection in the NMS-Free Era: A Comparative Performance Evaluation of YOLOv8 and YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24831)
*Chidera G. Oguine,Kanyifeechukwu J. Oguine,Obiozor M. Oguine,Ozioma C. Oguine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了基于NMS的YOLOv8与无NMS的YOLO26在通用目标检测（Pascal VOC）和密集航拍小目标检测（VisDrone）上的性能，涵盖五个尺度规模，评估指标包括精度、定位、模型大小、GFLOPs及CPU/GPU延迟。结果表明YOLO26在Pascal VOC上多数尺度表现更优且复杂度更低，但在VisDrone上两者差距缩小；YOLOv8在GPU延迟上仍具竞争力，无NMS设计并非普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMS作为实时目标检测的关键后处理步骤，在资源受限场景中会引入延迟波动和部署复杂性。尽管YOLO26等无NMS设计通过端到端检测试图降低这种依赖，但其与YOLOv8等基于NMS的经典模型在标准基准之外的系统性比较尚不充分，缺乏跨不同检测场景（通用检测 vs. 密集小目标检测）的多维度评估。

Method: 选取YOLOv8（NMS-based）和YOLO26（NMS-free）两大家族模型，在Pascal VOC（通用目标检测）和VisDrone（密集航拍小目标检测）两个数据集上，分别对五个不同规模（nano/ small/ medium/ large/ xlarge）的模型进行系统比较。评估指标涵盖检测精度（mAP）、定位精度、模型参数量、GFLOPs计算量以及CPU和GPU推理延迟。

Result: YOLO26在Pascal VOC上多数尺度下检测性能更强、模型复杂度更低；在VisDrone上两者性能差距缩小，均对密集小目标检测困难；YOLOv8在GPU延迟方面仍具优势，无NMS设计并未在所有部署场景中带来普遍优势。

Conclusion: 检测器选择需综合考虑数据集特性、目标尺度、模型容量和硬件约束，无NMS设计并非万能解决方案，NMS-based与NMS-free模型各有适用场景。

Abstract: Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) remains a key post-processing step in many real-time object detection pipelines, but it can introduce latency variation and deployment complexity in resource-constrained settings. Recent NMS-free designs such as YOLO26 aim to reduce this dependence through end-to-end detection, yet their performance relative to established NMS-based models such as YOLOv8 remains underexplored beyond standard benchmarks. This paper compares YOLOv8 and YOLO26 on Pascal VOC and VisDrone, representing general object detection and dense aerial small-object detection, respectively. Both model families are evaluated across five scales using accuracy, localization, model size, GFLOPs, and CPU/GPU latency. Results show that YOLO26 achieves stronger detection performance and lower model complexity on Pascal VOC across most scales, while the performance gap narrows on VisDrone, where both models struggle with dense small targets. YOLOv8 remains competitive in GPU latency, showing that NMS-free design does not guarantee universal deployment superiority. Overall, the study shows that detector selection depends on dataset characteristics, object scale, model capacity, and hardware constraints.

</details>


### [82] [Trajectory-Consistent Calibration for Cache-Accelerated Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24870)
*Mingyu Liang,Dingkun Xu,Jingwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹一致校准（TCC），一种无需训练的缓存校准方法，通过离线迭代过程校正扩散Transformer中缓存表示与完整计算之间的偏差，在保持复用策略的同时提升生成质量，在PixArt-alpha和DiT-XL/2上显著改善FID指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在迭代采样中需要重复进行去噪器评估，计算成本高昂。基于缓存的加速方法虽然通过复用中间表示降低了计算开销，但会引入表示偏差并降低生成质量。

Method: 提出Trajectory-Consistent Calibration (TCC)，一种无需训练的校准方法。TCC采用离线迭代过程，使每个校准先验都能考虑先前校准所引起的轨迹偏移，而不是从单个未校正的缓存轨迹中估计所有校准先验。

Result: 在PixArt-alpha和DiT-XL/2上，TCC一致提升了多种代表性缓存加速方法的FID指标。在基于FORA的PixArt-alpha缓存加速设置中，TCC将FID从29.83降至27.35，略微超过完整计算基线。

Conclusion: TCC通过轨迹一致的校准策略，有效缓解了缓存表示与完整计算之间的偏差，在不改变底层复用策略的前提下提升了生成质量，甚至在某些设置下超越了完整计算基线。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers require repeated denoiser evaluations during iterative sampling, making inference computationally expensive. Cache-based acceleration reduces this cost by reusing intermediate representations across denoising steps, but can introduce representation deviations and degrade generation quality. In this paper, we analyze these deviations and show that effective calibration should consider both the direct mismatch caused by reuse and the subsequent trajectory shift induced by earlier corrections. To address this challenge, we propose Trajectory-Consistent Calibration (TCC), a training-free method that calibrates cached representations toward their full-computation counterparts. Specifically, rather than estimating all calibration priors from a single uncorrected cache trajectory, TCC uses an offline iterative procedure so that each prior accounts for the trajectory shift induced by preceding calibrations. Experiments on PixArt-alpha and DiT-XL/2 show that TCC consistently improves FID across representative cache-based acceleration methods while preserving their underlying reuse policies. Notably, in a representative PixArt-alpha cache-acceleration setting based on FORA, TCC reduces FID from 29.83 to 27.35, slightly surpassing the full-computation baseline.

</details>


### [83] [QuoVLA: Quotient Space for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24890)
*Xuan Wang,Yinan Wu,Haoran Duan,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出商空间理论（Quotient Theory）证明预训练VLM潜变量已包含足够动作信息但存在过度完备性，并基于此设计QuoVLA框架，通过量化模块和双分支结构压缩表征，在机器人控制中取得优异泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型普遍认为预训练VLM潜变量缺乏可直接使用的动作信息，或应避免其受动作学习信号干扰。作者反对这一"动作不足"观点，试图证明VLM潜变量实际上是"动作充足"的，只是存在过度完备性——即区分了导致相同最优行为的提示级变体。

Method: 提出QuoVLA商空间框架：1) 量化模块（quantization module）压缩VLM潜变量；2) 双分支设计（dual-branch design）配合相对时间复杂度正则化（relative temporal-complexity regularization），保留动作相关信息同时去除提示级冗余。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，QuoVLA取得了强性能，特别是在视觉、语言和环境分布偏移下的泛化能力有显著提升。

Conclusion: 预训练VLM潜变量不是动作不足而是动作充足但过度完备，通过商空间压缩可有效提取动作相关信息，提升VLA模型的控制性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly adapt pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to robot control by mapping visual observations and language instructions to continuous actions. Existing approaches typically take an action-insufficiency view, assuming that pretrained VLM latents either lack directly usable action information or should be shielded from action-learning signals. Against this view, our \textit{Quotient Theory for VLA} shows that pretrained VLM latents are not action-insufficient but action-sufficient: they already contain the information needed for control, yet remain overcomplete by distinguishing prompt-level variations that induce the same optimal action behavior. To operationalize this theory, we propose QuoVLA, a quotient-space framework for VLA that compresses pretrained VLM latents into action-sufficient representations. Specifically, QuoVLA instantiates this principle with a quantization module and a dual-branch design with relative temporal-complexity regularization, preserving action-relevant information while removing prompt-level redundancy. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that QuoVLA achieves strong performance, with particularly notable improvements in generalization under visual, linguistic, and environmental distribution shifts. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [84] [X-Foresight: A Joint Vision-Action Causal Forecasting Network via Predictive World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24892)
*Baolu Li,Jingyu Qian,Rui Guo,Yilun Chen,Hanpeng Liu,Yuan Lin,Junhong Zhou,Ruixin Liu,Willow Yang,Yutong Zheng,Zhenli Zhang,Tenglong,Gu,Zhuangzhuang Ding,Pengkun Zheng,Yu Zhang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Foresight将预测世界模型直接集成到VLA架构中，通过长跨度分块自回归策略、课程学习和时间重要性采样，解决了视频预测中的低熵冗余和长程因果建模难题，显著提升了规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界知识主要蕴含在视频中，使VLA模型具备这种知识对于安全且泛化的规划至关重要。然而，朴素的下一个帧预测面临两个挑战：1）视频token是低熵且冗余的，导致预测退化为平凡外推；2）世界建模存在时间困境：密集预测捕捉瞬时动态，但无法高效建模长程因果关系。

Method: 1) 长跨度分块自回归策略：预测语义上相距较远的分块而非相邻帧，同时保留分块内密集帧（瞬时动态）和分块间稀疏过渡（长程因果）；2) 课程学习：逐步延长预测视野并稳定长程训练；3) 时间重要性采样：根据自我运动和行为信号识别安全关键分块，集中监督；4) 扩散式多视角渲染器：提升逼真度。

Result: X-Foresight在规划性能上显著优于VLA基线，同时保持良好的生成逼真度。

Conclusion: X-Foresight建立了世界知识驱动的自主系统的稳健范式。

Abstract: Physical world knowledge resides mainly in videos. Equipping Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with such knowledge is fundamental for safe and generalizable planning. Predictive world modeling enables VLA to internalize physical dynamics and long-term causality by predicting future video from past observations. However, naive next-frame prediction faces two challenges: 1) unlike semantically distinct text tokens, video tokens are low-entropy and redundant, causing prediction to degenerate into trivial extrapolation. 2) world modeling poses a temporal dilemma: dense prediction captures instantaneous dynamics, but cannot efficiently model long-horizon causality.
  To learn world knowledge effectively, we introduce X-Foresight, a predictive world model integrated directly into the VLA architecture to jointly learn world modeling and real-time action control. At its core lies a long-horizon chunk-wise auto-regressive strategy that addresses both challenges: by predicting semantically distant chunks rather than adjacent frames, it escapes trivial extrapolation, while preserving dense intra-chunk frames for instantaneous dynamics and sparse inter-chunk transitions for long-term causality. A curriculum learning schedule progressively extends prediction horizons and stabilizes long-horizon training. To capture long-term causality effectively, we present temporal importance sampling, which concentrates supervision on safety-critical chunks identified by ego-motion and behavioral signals. We further delegate photorealistic synthesis to a diffusion-based multi-view renderer, improving photorealistic appearance.
  Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that X-Foresight significantly outperforms VLA baselines in planning performance while maintaining strong generative fidelity, establishing a robust paradigm for world-knowledge-driven autonomous systems.

</details>


### [85] [BED-SAM2: Boundary-Enhanced-Depth SAM2 via Monocular Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24893)
*Tyler Rust,Dara McNally,Kyle O'Donnell,Colin Kelly,Chandra Kambhamettu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BED-SAM2，通过修改SAM2编码器直接编码单目深度信息，增强几何线索以改善目标边界描绘和伪装物体形状提取，在多个显著和伪装目标检测任务中以仅5个训练周期达到竞争性SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升SAM2在下游分割任务中的性能，尤其是改善目标边界描绘和伪装物体形状提取，通过引入单目深度信息作为几何线索来增强模型能力。

Method: 修改SAM2 Hiera编码器架构，使其能够直接从RGB图像中编码单目深度信息，从而提供几何线索来增强目标边界描绘和伪装物体形状提取。

Result: 在多个显著和伪装目标检测任务中，仅需5个训练周期即达到具有竞争力的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: BED-SAM2通过引入深度信息有效提升了SAM2在下游分割任务中的边界描绘和伪装物体检测能力，且训练效率高。

Abstract: Building upon the SAM2 vision foundation model for downstream segmentation, this study introduces Boundary Enhanced Depth (BED)-SAM2. The SAM2 Hiera encoder architecture is modified to directly encode monocular depth information from RGB images, thereby providing geometric cues that enhance object boundary delineation and facilitate the extraction of camouflaged object shapes. BED-SAM2 demonstrates competitive state-of-the-art performance across multiple salient and camouflaged object detection tasks with as few as five training epochs.

</details>


### [86] [BFS: Back-to-Front Layered Image Synthesis via Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24894)
*Kyoungkook Kang,Gyujin Sim,Sunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BFS提出了一种基于生成的双分支扩散框架，通过利用无分层图像的合成知识来解决分层图像合成中的数据稀缺问题，在前后景分离与视觉融合方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层图像合成方法中，基于分解的方法难以实现干净的前后景分离，而基于生成的方法则受限于训练数据获取困难，导致生成质量和场景多样性不足。

Method: 提出了BFS框架：采用双分支扩散架构，两个相互关联的分支分别生成合成图像和前景层，实现双向知识迁移；并设计了两阶段训练方案，利用高质量的无分层合成图像数据集来增强前景生成质量。

Result: 大量实验和用户研究表明，BFS能够生成高质量的分层图像，在前后景分离、视觉效果（阴影、反射）融合以及整体合成质量上持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用易于学习的无分层图像合成知识来指导分层前景合成，BFS有效克服了数据稀缺问题，为可控且高质量的分层图像合成提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: As generative models expand the possibilities of visual content creation, layered image synthesis has emerged as a promising direction for controllable and creative editing. However, existing methods struggle to fully realize this potential. Decomposition-based methods often struggle with clean separation, while generation-based methods suffer from difficulty in training data acquisition, reducing quality and scene diversity. In this paper, we propose BFS, a novel generation-based framework for layered image synthesis. Specifically, given a background image and user guidance, BFS synthesizes a foreground layer that incorporates not only a foreground object but also its associated visual effects, such as shadows and reflections, while seamlessly harmonizing with the background to produce a coherent composite. To enable diverse and high-quality foreground layer synthesis while overcoming data scarcity, we leverage the comparatively easy-to-learn knowledge of unlayered image synthesis for the foreground synthesis. To this end, we adopt a dual-branch diffusion framework in which two interconnected branches generate a composite image and a foreground layer, respectively, enabling bidirectional knowledge transfer. Based on this framework, we propose a two-stage training scheme that utilizes a high-quality unlayered composite image dataset to effectively enhance foreground quality. Extensive experiments, including a user study, show that BFS produces high-quality layered images, consistently outperforming prior methods.

</details>


### [87] [Where Detectors Fail: Probing Generative Space for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24906)
*Zijie Cao,Weijie Tu,Yao Xiao,Weijian Deng,Liang Lin,Pengxu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROBE是一个通过主动探索生成过程困难区域来提升AI生成图像检测器泛化能力的框架，通过在生成器中进行流形级修改产生难以分类的样本，从而改进检测器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在生成设置改变时性能严重下降，仅靠增大训练数据规模不足以解决泛化问题，关键原因在于训练时对生成变异的覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PROBE框架，将检测器作为评判器引导生成器进行流形级修改，主动探索生成过程中具有挑战性的区域，产生常规采样策略下难以出现的困难样本，用于优化检测器。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验结果表明，PROBE框架能够显著提升检测器对未见生成器的泛化能力，实现更可泛化的AIGI检测性能。

Conclusion: 主动探索生成过程的困难区域而非被动依赖固定数据源，是提升AIGI检测器泛化性的有效途径，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated images (AIGI) remains challenging because detectors often fail to generalize to unseen generators. Although existing methods are trained on large datasets, their performance still degrades when generation settings change, indicating that data scale alone is insufficient and that limited coverage of generative variations during training is a key factor. Studies on generative model editing show that small changes in internal representations can produce diverse and meaningful image variations, many of which are not explored under standard sampling. Leveraging this insight, we propose PROBE (Probing Robustness via Boundary Exploration), a framework that improves detector generalization by actively exploring challenging regions of the generative process. Instead of treating the generator as a fixed data source, PROBE uses the detector as a critic to steer the generator through manifold-level modifications, producing realistic samples that are difficult to classify. These samples expose failure cases that are uncommon under standard data sampling strategies and are used to refine the detector. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks indicate that PROBE enhances generalization to unseen generators, resulting in more generalizable AIGI detection performance. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Amamiya-C/PROBE-AIGI-Detection

</details>


### [88] [X-Edit: Exact, Explicit, and Explainable Null-Space Editing for Medical Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24932)
*Yuanye Liu,Siyuan Zhou,Ke Zhang,Lei Li,Wei Chen,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Edit提出了一种基于零空间投影的模型编辑框架，通过闭式解精确修正Vision Transformer在医学图像分类中的错误预测，同时数学上保证不会破坏已建立的诊断知识，在六个医学影像基准上有效抑制了灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微调方法在修正预训练ViT的失败案例时存在灾难性遗忘问题，导致已有诊断能力严重退化，这在临床场景中存在安全隐患。现有方法缺乏一种主动、可控、可靠且具备理论依据和可解释性的干预机制。

Method: X-Edit包含两步：首先通过因果追踪显式定位控制错误预测的关键层；然后从锚定集构建正交零空间投影矩阵，将参数更新几何约束在该零空间内，实现精确修正而不扰动已有诊断表征的闭式解。

Result: 在六个医学影像基准数据集上的评估表明，X-Edit全面抑制了灾难性遗忘，同时取得了优异的编辑成功率。

Conclusion: X-Edit提供了一种高效、可解释且理论完备的模型编辑方案，为ViT在临床中的安全部署提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: Pre-trained Vision Transformers (ViTs) are increasingly deployed for medical image classification. However, correcting their inevitable failure cases in dynamic clinical scenarios poses a critical challenge. Conventional fine-tuning approaches inherently suffer from catastrophic forgetting, severely degrading previously acquired diagnostic capabilities. Such instability fundamentally compromises clinical safety. Addressing this vulnerability requires an active, controllable, and reliable intervention mechanism that is both theoretically grounded and inherently interpretable. To this end, we propose X-Edit (eXact, eXplicit, and eXplainable Editing), an efficient null-space model editing framework. X-Edit transitions the editing process from iterative gradient-based optimization to a theoretically grounded, closed-form solution. Specifically, we first explicitly localize the influential layers via causal tracing governing the erroneous prediction. Subsequently, we construct an orthogonal null-space projection matrix from a curated anchor set. By geometrically constraining the exact parameter update strictly within this null space, we provide mathematical guarantees that the intervention rectifies targeted errors without perturbing established diagnostic representations. Extensive evaluations on six medical imaging benchmarks demonstrate that X-Edit comprehensively suppresses catastrophic forgetting while achieving superior edit success rates. Our code is available at https://github.com/HenryLau7/X-Edit.

</details>


### [89] [Interpretability Transfer from Language to Vision via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24946)
*Alexey Kravets,Da Li,Chuan Li,Da Chen,Vinay P. Namboodiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA通过将视觉token映射到语言模型预训练的有标签文本SAE空间，实现了无需训练专用视觉SAE的可解释性，相比视觉基线在物体移除/替换任务上分别提升35%/47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）在语言模型可解释性方面取得进展，但难以有效迁移到视觉领域，主要原因是视觉概念标注困难且存在歧义。作者希望在不训练专用视觉SAE的前提下，实现视觉可解释性。

Method: 提出VISTA框架，在LLaVA风格视觉-语言模型中，通过约束视觉投影器将视觉token映射到LLM预先存在且已标注的文本SAE空间，并利用LLM的SAE重建损失对投影器进行正则化。

Result: 匹配率提升三倍；DINOv2特征定位能力优于其他视觉编码器；在细粒度局部概念干预中，物体移除任务提升35%，物体替换任务提升47%。

Conclusion: VISTA成功实现了从语言到视觉的可解释性迁移，提供了视觉token存在于文本SAE流形上的因果证据，且在多种LLM架构上得到验证。

Abstract: Recent advances in language model interpretability using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have yet to effectively translate to the visual domain, mainly due to the difficulty and ambiguity of labeling visual concepts. In this paper, we introduce Visual Interpretability via SAE Transfer Alignment (VISTA), a framework that transfers interpretability from language to vision in a LLaVA-style vision-language model by constraining a visual projector to map visual tokens into an LLM's pre-existing, labeled textual SAE space. This approach enables visual interpretability without training dedicated vision SAEs. By regularizing the projector using the LLM's SAE reconstruction loss, VISTA achieves a threefold increase in the matching rate, which measures how accurately the most activating textual concepts in the SAE space correspond to semantic elements in the image. Using this framework, we further analyze spatial localization properties of different vision encoders and show that DINOv2 features have stronger localization abilities than other encoders. Leveraging this precision, we validate VISTA's cross-modal alignment through fine-grained, localized concept interventions, where specific objects are removed or replaced in the model's perception while preserving the surrounding scene. This results in improvements of 35% in object removal and 47% in object replacement tasks over vision-only baselines, providing causal evidence that visual tokens inhabit the text SAE manifold. These contributions are validated across multiple LLM architectures.

</details>


### [90] [Three-Step Conditional Diffusion 3D Reconstruction for Light-Field Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24959)
*Qihong Zhao,Shaokang Yan,Zhimin Qiao,Jinjia Wang,Bo Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三步条件扩散模型（TCD），用于光场显微镜的高保真三维重建，通过确定性三步采样策略和轻量级条件U-Net解决了传统扩散模型采样慢、质量与效率权衡的问题，并引入类间检测模块增强模型稳定性，实验表明在重建保真度和泛化能力上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光场显微镜可实现单次拍摄多角度信息并支持实时体积成像，但传统物理算法受限于空间分辨率低、伪影严重和计算成本高，现有学习方法在重建精度和泛化能力上仍存在不足。

Method: 提出三步条件扩散模型（TCD），采用确定性三步采样策略加速推理，结合轻量级条件U-Net实现快速精确的体积重建；同时引入类间检测（ICD）模块，在推理时识别分布外或异常输入以增强模型稳定性和可靠性。

Result: 大量实验和跨数据集评估表明，TCD在重建保真度和泛化能力上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: TCD为光场显微镜提供了一种高效、实用的3D重建方案，在速度和精度之间建立了新的平衡范式。

Abstract: Light-field microscopy (LFM) enables single-shot capture of multi-angular information from biological samples, supporting real-time volumetric imaging. However, traditional physics-based algorithms often suffer from limited spatial resolution, severe artifacts, and high computational costs. Existing learning-based methods improve inference efficiency but still face limitations in reconstruction accuracy and generalization capability. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a high-fidelity Three-Step Conditional Diffusion (TCD) 3D reconstruction method for LFM. Although conventional diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative modeling, their slow sampling process and the inherent trade-off between quality and efficiency hinder their application in real-time 3D imaging. We redesign the diffusion process through a deterministic three-step sampling strategy coupled with a lightweight conditional U-Net, establishing a new paradigm for fast and accurate volumetric reconstruction. Furthermore, an Inter-Class Detection (ICD) module is incorporated to identify out-of-distribution or anomalous inputs during inference, thereby enhancing model stability and reliability. Extensive experiments and cross-dataset evaluations demonstrate that TCD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both reconstruction fidelity and generalization, providing an efficient and practical 3D reconstruction solution for light-field microscopy.

</details>


### [91] [Tempered Self-Similarity Alignment for Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24962)
*Manjin Kim,Suha Kwak,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将视觉基础模型中的时空自相似性（STSS）关系知识转移到视频生成模型中，并引入温和自相似性对齐（TSA）损失函数，显著提升了视频生成的物理真实感，在VideoPhy和VideoPhy2基准上取得了大幅改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在生成物理真实的视频方面表现不佳，常出现外观漂移、不合理运动和时间不一致等问题，亟需解决这些物理合理性缺陷。

Method: 利用视觉基础模型中编码的时空自相似性（STSS）关系知识，将STSS转换为概率对应分布，提出温和自相似性对齐（TSA）损失，在动态变化区域对齐视频生成模型与视觉基础模型的对应分布。

Result: 在VideoPhy和VideoPhy2基准测试上，该方法在多种交互场景下显著提升了物理合理性。

Conclusion: 转移关系知识是提升视频生成物理真实感的有效方法，验证了STSS和TSA在物理合理视频生成中的价值。

Abstract: Despite remarkable advances in video generative models, they still struggle to generate physically realistic videos, frequently exhibiting appearance drift, implausible motion, and temporal inconsistencies. In this work, we address this limitation by transferring relational knowledge encoded in spatio-temporal self-similarity (STSS) from visual foundation models into video generative models. STSS represents pairwise similarities among features across space and time, revealing the relational structure of how objects interact with other entities throughout a video, effectively capturing real-world dynamics, including object motion and semantic transformations. To transfer this relational knowledge, we propose Tempered Self-similarity Alignment (TSA) loss, which transforms STSS into probabilistic correspondence distributions and trains the video generative model to align its correspondence distributions with those of the visual foundation model on dynamically changing regions. Evaluated on VideoPhy and VideoPhy2 benchmarks, our method demonstrates substantial improvements in physical plausibility across diverse interaction scenarios, validating the effectiveness of transferring relational knowledge for physically realistic video generation.

</details>


### [92] [ConFi-GS Confidence-Guided High-Frequency Injection for 3D Gaussian Splatting Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24964)
*Jiaxiang Li,Zongtan Zhou,Zhen Tan,Yadong Liu,Dewen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低分辨率多视图图像重建3D场景时，现有3DGS方法无法区分"哪里需要细节"和"候选高频内容是否可靠"这两个问题。本文提出可靠性感知频率建模框架，通过几何引导的细节需求先验和频率感知可靠性图，生成细节注入图，并结合空间选择性监督、由粗到细的频率正则化和可靠性感知高斯致密化，在多个基准上提升了保真度和感知质量，抑制了不稳定和视图不一致的细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在低分辨率重建中要么统一应用超分辨率引导，要么仅基于几何采样定位增强区域，未能区分"哪些区域需要额外细节"和"候选高频内容是否足够可靠以融入多视图一致的3D表示"这两个根本不同的问题。

Method: 提出可靠性感知频率建模框架：(1) 几何引导的细节需求先验，定位低分辨率监督下可能欠细节的区域；(2) 频率感知可靠性图，判断候选高频细节是否结构支撑、频谱未解、跨视图稳定；(3) 结合两者生成细节注入图，指导超分辨率细节的引入位置；(4) 统一优化方案，包括空间选择性监督、由粗到细频率正则化、可靠性感知高斯致密化。

Result: 在多个基准上，该方法相比现有方法提升了重建的保真度和感知质量，同时有效抑制了不稳定或视图不一致的细节伪影。

Conclusion: 通过区分"需要细节"和"细节的可靠性"两个问题，并设计对应的可靠性感知频率建模与优化框架，能够从低分辨率多视图图像中重建出高质量、多视图一致的3D场景。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-quality 3D scenes from low-resolution multi-view images remains challenging for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), because insufficient high-frequency observations often lead to blurred textures, weak boundaries, and view-inconsistent details. Existing approaches either apply super-resolution guidance uniformly or localize enhancement regions based mainly on geometric sampling. However, they typically do not distinguish between two fundamentally different questions: where additional detail is needed, and whether the corresponding candidate high-frequency content is reliable enough to be internalized into a multi-view consistent 3D representation.
  In this paper, we propose a reliability-aware frequency modeling framework for low-resolution 3DGS reconstruction. The framework first estimates a geometry-guided detail-demand prior to locate regions that are likely under-detailed under low-resolution supervision. It then computes a frequency-aware reliability map to determine whether candidate high-frequency details are structurally supported, spectrally unresolved, and cross-view stable. Combining these signals yields a detail-injection map that guides where super-resolved details should be introduced during optimization. Based on this map, we design a unified optimization scheme comprising spatially selective supervision, coarse-to-fine frequency regularization, and reliability-aware Gaussian densification. This scheme controls where reliable details are injected, when high-frequency supervision is activated, and how unresolved yet reliable details are internalized into the Gaussian representation. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show improved fidelity and perceptual quality while suppressing unstable or view-inconsistent details.

</details>


### [93] [MinerU-Popo: Universal Post-Processing Model for Structured Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24973)
*Bangrui Xu,Ziyang Miao,Xuanhe Zhou,Yiming Lin,Zirui Tang,Xiaomeng Zhao,Fan Wu,Cheng Tan,Fan Wu,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MinerU-Popo，一个轻量级通用框架，通过后处理将OCR输出的页面级结果重构为连贯的文档级结构，包含文本/表格截断恢复、标题层级重建和图文关联四个子任务，显著提升标题层级TEDS和RAG准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM-based OCR模型擅长页面级元素提取（如单页内的段落及其边界框和文本），但下游应用（如RAG）需要连贯的文档级信息，而OCR模型往往破坏跨页连续性，无法恢复被分页截断的段落、表格等结构。这些跨页关系需要联合分析多页中的标题、段落、表格和图像，因此需要重用现有OCR输出，通过后处理重建文档级逻辑结构。

Method: 将问题分解为四个子任务：文本截断恢复、表格截断恢复、标题层级重建和图文关联。构建任务导向的数据引擎（含任务特定输入过滤），生成30K数据微调轻量级后处理模型Qwen3-VL-4B。引入动态分块与重叠同步机制，对齐分块级输出并保持全局一致性，最终组装为树状文档表示，并附加节点分块和摘要以支持下游检索与分析。

Result: 在五种OCR模型上，标题层级TEDS提升至少20%，RAG准确率提高，单查询延迟降低。

Conclusion: MinerU-Popo作为轻量级通用后处理框架，能有效将多种OCR解析器的页面级输出转换为连贯的文档级结构，在标题层级重建、RAG准确性和延迟方面均取得显著改进。

Abstract: VLM-based OCR models have become the de facto choice for document parsing, as they can accurately extract page-level elements (e.g., paragraphs within individual pages) together with their bounding boxes and textual content. However, downstream applications such as RAG require coherent document-level information, whereas these models often break cross-page continuity and fail to recover disrupted structures, such as paragraphs and tables truncated by page boundaries. Such relationships are not confined to a single page; instead, they require joint analysis of titles, paragraphs, tables, and images spanning multiple pages. A natural solution is therefore to reuse existing OCR outputs and reconstruct document-level logical structures through post-processing.
  To this end, we propose MinerU-Popo, a lightweight and universal framework for POst-Processing OCR outputs, which converts page-level results from diverse parsers into coherent document-level structures. MinerU-Popo decomposes the problem into four focused subtasks: text truncation recovery, table truncation recovery, title hierarchy reconstruction, and image-text association. To address these effectively, we build a task-oriented data engine with task-specific input filtering, and use the generated data (30K) to fine-tune a lightweight post-processing model (Qwen3-VL-4B). To support long documents, we introduce dynamic chunking with overlap-based synchronization, which aligns chunk-level outputs from the fine-tuned model and preserves global consistency. Finally, we assemble the aligned outputs into a tree-structured document representation, further enriched with node chunking and summaries for downstream retrieval and analysis. Empirical results show MinerU-Popo improves title-hierarchy TEDS by at least 20% across all five tested OCR models, improves RAG accuracy and reduces per-query latency.

</details>


### [94] [Universal Boosts, Specific Suppressors: Sparse Autoencoder Steering of Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24977)
*Farhad Nooralahzadeh,Benjamin Gundersen,Nicolas Deperrois,Hidetoshi Matsuom,Mizuho Nishio,Thomas Frauenfelder,Ahmed Allam,Christian Blüthgen,Michael Moor,Michael Krauthammer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需权重更新的解码时残差引导方法，通过逐token稀疏自编码器（SAE）对医学视觉语言模型（VLM）生成的胸部X光报告进行因果纠偏，在MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray数据集上显著提升了报告质量，减少了幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM在生成胸部X光报告时经常出现幻觉（虚构、遗漏或错误定位发现），现有方法需要权重更新或重新训练，缺乏高效的推理时干预手段。

Method: 在推理阶段，对模型后期层的Top-K SAE进行解码时残差引导：首先识别与临床错误相关的因果特征方向，然后在生成过程中对促进质量的方向进行增强（boost），对导致幻觉的方向进行抑制（suppress），实现无需权重更新的干预。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR上，对RadVLM、LLaVA-Rad和CheXOne三个模型，临床复合指标分别相对提升+5.4%、+7.2%和+17.0%，GREEN指标均显著提升。零样本迁移至IU-Xray也获得+7.7%的GREEN相对提升。

Conclusion: 跨模型特征对齐表明，促进质量的方向在不同架构间高度重叠，而抑制幻觉的方向是模型特异的。因此可迁移的引导策略需要针对每个骨架单独处理抑制方向，而非共享通用抑制列表。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) often hallucinate findings when generating chest X-ray reports: they fabricate findings that are not present in the image, miss important ones, or locate them incorrectly. We mitigate this without weight updates by decoding-time residual steering on a per-token sparse autoencoder (SAE) basis: Top-$K$ SAEs on late layers, causal steering against clinical errors, then combined suppress/boost intervention at inference time. On the MIMIC-CXR test split, our inference-only method improves the quality of generated reports for three radiology VLMs (RadVLM, LLaVA-Rad, and CheXOne), with relative improvements of +5.4%, +7.2%, and +17.0% in the clinical composite metric, and statistically significant GREEN gains on all backbones. A cross-model feature alignment shows that the quality-promoting (boost) directions overlap strongly across architectures, whereas hallucination-linked (suppress) directions are model-specific. Therefore, transferable steering must treat suppression per-backbone, rather than sharing a universal suppress list. The same recipe transfers zero-shot to IU-Xray (Green $+7.7\%$ rel.) without retraining, confirming that the identified features are properties of the model, not of the training corpus. We release causal feature sets and an interactive feature dashboard: https://cxr-sparse-feature-dashboard.netlify.app/.

</details>


### [95] [ClueAegis: Heuristic-to-Reasoning Cognitive-skill Learning for Unified Evidence-based Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25009)
*Huangsen Cao,Hongkang Chu,Yuxi Li,Ying Zhang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Yongwei Wang,Yu Zhao,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种从认知角度出发的启发式到推理（Heuristic-to-Reasoning）技能学习框架ClueAegis，将合成图像检测重构为多技能推理过程，并构建了结构化评估基准ClueAegis-Bench，在跨域泛化、鲁棒性和可解释性上优于传统端到端方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成图像检测方法局限于端到端分类或单一推理，无法建模结构化的取证推理过程与异构视觉证据，缺乏可解释性和认知层面的推理能力。

Method: 提出ClueAegis两阶段智能体框架：首先通过启发式线索提取选择最优取证技能，然后基于技能条件执行证据引导的推理（skill-conditioned toolchains），将检测转化为可配置的多技能推理过程，并配套构建了ClueAegis-Bench基准用于结构化评估。

Result: 在多项实验中达到最先进性能，跨域泛化能力和鲁棒性均优于现有方法，同时提供透明的推理轨迹和结构化取证证据。

Conclusion: ClueAegis通过将合成图像检测重构为认知技能驱动的可解释推理过程，有效弥补了传统端到端方法在结构化取证和可解释性上的不足，为可解释的合成图像检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models has made synthetic images increasingly realistic, challenging reliable detection. Existing methods are often limited to end-to-end classification or monolithic reasoning, and thus fail to model structured forensic reasoning and heterogeneous visual evidence. We revisit synthetic image detection from a cognitive perspective and propose a \textit{Heuristic-to-Reasoning} cognitive skill learning framework for evidence-based forensic analysis. Given an input image, our framework first extracts heuristic perceptual clues, selects the optimal forensic skill, and then performs skill-conditioned reasoning for evidence extraction and decision making. To support this paradigm, we introduce \textbf{ClueAegis-Bench}, which decomposes synthetic image detection into explicitly annotated forensic cognitive skills for structured evaluation beyond binary classification. Based on this benchmark, we propose \textbf{ClueAegis} (\underline{C}ognitive-skill \underline{L}earning for \underline{U}nified \underline{E}vidence-based Synthetic Image Detection), a two-stage agentic framework that conducts heuristic skill selection followed by evidence-guided reasoning through skill-conditioned toolchains. This design reformulates synthetic image detection as a configurable multi-skill reasoning process that bridges perception, skill selection, and forensic reasoning. Extensive experiments show that ClueAegis achieves state-of-the-art performance while improving cross-domain generalization and robustness. It also provides transparent reasoning trajectories and structured forensic evidence, offering a more explainable alternative to conventional end-to-end detectors.

</details>


### [96] [Learning from Semantic Dictionaries: Discriminative Codebook Contrastive Learning for Unified Visual Representation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25012)
*Imanol G. Estepa,Jesús M Rodríguez-de-Vera,Bhalaji Nagarajan,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEASE是一种自监督框架，通过配对生成-判别式码本设计，在离散令牌空间中联合优化掩码重建损失和码本对比损失，实现视觉理解与生成的统一，在ImageNet-1K上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判别式与生成式视觉模型各自表现优异但存在语义不对齐，阻碍了统一视觉学习的发展。

Method: 提出LEASE框架，采用配对生成-判别式码本设计，在一次性预计算的离散令牌空间中运作；集成两个互补目标：捕获细粒度生成细节的掩码令牌重建损失，以及通过自适应质心加权对齐判别式语义的码本对比损失。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，LEASE在linear probing（+1.7%）、无条件生成（FID -1.26, IS +10.19）、少样本学习（+0.56%）、迁移（+0.75%）和鲁棒性（+5.86%和+4.25%）等基准上超越MAGE和Sorcen等先前VQGAN方法。

Conclusion: LEASE为面向通用视觉模型的联合理解与生成提供了高效且有效的步骤。

Abstract: Discriminative and generative vision models excel in their respective domains but remain semantically misaligned, hindering progress toward unified visual learning. We introduce LEASE (LEArning from SEmantic Dictionaries), a self-supervised framework that bridges this gap using a paired generative-discriminative codebook design. LEASE operates entirely in a discrete token space produced through a one-time precomputation step, enabling efficient training without data augmentations, teacher models, or online tokenizers. LEASE integrates two complementary objectives: a masked token reconstruction loss that captures fine-grained generative detail, and a codebook contrast loss that aligns encoder features with discriminative semantics via adaptive centroid weighting. This dual supervision yields a unified latent space that supports both high-quality generation and strong representation learning. On ImageNet-1K, LEASE achieves state-of-the-art unified performance, outperforming prior VQGAN-based methods such as MAGE and Sorcen across linear probing (up to +1.7%), unconditional generation (-1.26 FID and +10.19 IS w.r.t MAGE), few-shot learning (+0.56% on average against Sorcen), transfer (+0.75% average improvement against MAGE and Sorcen), and robustness benchmarks (+5.86% and +4.25% average improvement against MAGE and Sorcen, respectively). It also competes favorably with domain-specialized contrastive and generative models while surpassing previous MIM methods. The unsupervised LEASE model can also be extended to conditional generation by building upon its learned representations, proving competitive with specialized baselines. Overall, LEASE provides an efficient and effective step toward general-purpose vision models that jointly understand and generate visual content.

</details>


### [97] [Stop Denoising Your Blurs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25014)
*Sasidhar Parvathireddy,Vamsidhar Saraswathula,Rama Krishna Gorthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConvDiff是一种基于卷积而非加性噪声的扩散模型框架，用于图像去模糊任务。它通过频域特性构建从清晰图像到模糊图像的前向轨迹，弥合了模糊原理与扩散模型之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型假设退化过程是加性噪声，但模糊模型本质上是卷积操作，违反了这个假设。作者旨在提出一种更符合物理原理的扩散框架来解决图像去模糊问题。

Method: 提出ConvDiff框架，用卷积操作替代传统扩散模型中的加性噪声操作。在前向过程中，利用频域分解特性构建从清晰图像到模糊图像的有意义的轨迹，而非逐步添加噪声。当前工作针对高斯模糊进行了实例化，其中频域分解可产生闭合形式且物理有效的中间状态。

Result: 该方法弥合了模糊的数学原理与基于扩散的重建算法迭代设计之间的鸿沟，实现了更具物理基础且更有效的图像恢复模型。

Conclusion: ConvDiff通过将扩散模型的前向过程从加性噪声改为卷积操作，更贴合模糊退化的物理本质。该原理可推广到其他类型的模糊，为图像恢复提供了更物理可信的框架。

Abstract: In recent times, diffusion models have achieved remarkable performance in image restoration tasks. Their core mechanism relies on the restricted presumption of degradation prior to the additive noise operation. However, the blur model, one of the most widely studied degradation formulations, violates this assumption, as it is inherently based on convolution rather than addition. In this paper, we introduce ConvDiff, a novel diffusion based framework that substitutes the additive operation with convolution for the task of image deblurring. In the forward process, we construct a meaningful trajectory from the clean image to its blurred counterpart by exploiting the frequency domain characteristics of convolution, rather than progressively corrupting the image with additive noise. While the current work instantiates this framework for Gaussian blur, where frequency-domain decomposition yields closed-form and physically valid intermediate states, the underlying principle of constructing degradation trajectories from the blur operator extends naturally to other blur families. This formulation bridges the gap between the mathematical principles of blurring and the iterative design of diffusion-based restoration algorithms, enabling more physically grounded and effective image restoration models.

</details>


### [98] [D3S2: Diffusion-Guided Dataset Distillation for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25022)
*Wenjie Zheng,Haoji Hu,Jiali Lu,Xingze Zou,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出D3S2框架，针对语义分割任务中的数据集蒸馏问题，通过类别平衡掩码选择和扩散引导图像合成两个阶段，在1%极低压缩率下较随机选择提升9.34%和5.70% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法主要关注图像分类，密集预测任务（如语义分割）中尚未被充分探索；语义分割的数据集蒸馏面临三个关键挑战：长尾类别不平衡、像素级严格对齐、高分辨率数据优化的高计算成本。

Method: 两阶段设计：1) 类别平衡掩码选择——采用贪心策略优先选择欠表示类别的代表性掩码；2) 扩散引导图像合成——利用预训练的布局到图像扩散模型，以所选掩码为条件生成图像，并通过引导扩散采样引入两个互补目标：分割一致性子损失（像素级对齐）和类别级特征匹配子损失（跨层对齐每类特征统计量）。

Result: 在ADE20K和COCO-Stuff数据集上，以1%压缩率使用Mask2Former（Swin-S）分别达到24.99%和35.49% mIoU，较随机选择提升9.34%和5.70%。

Conclusion: D3S2有效解决了语义分割数据集蒸馏的三大挑战，在极低压缩率下显著优于随机选择基线，验证了扩散模型在分割蒸馏中的潜力。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) aims to compress large-scale datasets into compact synthetic sets while preserving training efficacy. However, existing studies mainly focus on image classification, leaving dense prediction tasks such as semantic segmentation largely underexplored. In this work, we identify three key challenges for segmentation DD: (i) long-tailed class imbalance, (ii) the need for strict pixel-wise alignment between images and dense labels, and (iii) the high computational cost of optimizing high-resolution data with complex models. To address these challenges, we propose D3S2, a Diffusion-guided Dataset Distillation framework for Semantic Segmentation. Our method adopts a two-stage design. In Class-Balanced Mask Selection, we construct a representative mask set via a greedy strategy that prioritizes underrepresented classes. In Diffusion-Guided Image Synthesis, we employ a pretrained layout-to-image diffusion model to generate images conditioned on the selected masks, naturally ensuring spatial alignment. To further enhance the training utility of synthesized data, we introduce guided diffusion sampling with two complementary objectives: a segmentation-consistency loss for pixel-level alignment, and a class-wise feature matching loss for aligning per-class feature statistics across layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of D3S2. Notably, at an extremely compression rate of 1%, our method achieves 24.99% and 35.49% mIoU on ADE20K and COCO-Stuff with Mask2Former (Swin-S), outperforming random selection by 9.34% and 5.70%, respectively.

</details>


### [99] [AstroRAG -- A Pagerank-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation Pipeline for Question Answering in Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25039)
*Zhifeng Wang,Jason Jingshi Li,Kaihao Zhang,Ramesh Sankaranarayana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AstroRAG，一种基于 PageRank 重排序的天文学问答 RAG 流水线，通过令牌感知分块、Elasticsearch 临时索引、MMR 初选和 PageRank 精选的两阶段检索策略，在 AstroQA 基准上将 Mistral-7B 的准确率和 F1 分数提升至 79.49%，几乎是非 RAG 版本的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在依赖参数化知识时经常产生事实错误，传统 RAG 的“检索-转储”方式会引入不相关的上下文，降低答案质量。因此，需要一种针对天文学领域的、能够精确选择支持性上下文的 RAG 流水线。

Method: 系统首先进行令牌感知的文本分块，在 Elasticsearch 中构建每个实例的临时索引（防止跨任务泄漏）；然后执行两阶段检索：第一阶段用最大边际相关性（MMR）获得少量多样的候选集，第二阶段在相似性图上运行基于阅读器的 PageRank（PR）重排序，在严格令牌预算下选出紧凑且相互支持的上下文。整个设计无需训练、保护隐私且完全可复现。

Result: 在 AstroQA 天文学问答基准上，RAG 增强的 Mistral-7B 在所有难度级别上均表现优异，准确率和 F1 分数均达到 79.49%，几乎是非 RAG 版本的两倍。

Conclusion: 有纪律的检索与精炼策略能有效提升领域特定推理能力，为将 RAG 扩展到其他科学领域奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong performance in natural language processing but often generate factual errors when relying solely on parametric knowledge. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates these errors by grounding responses in external evidence, yet conventional retrieve-and-dump approaches frequently introduce irrelevant context that degrades answer quality. In this work, we present AstroRAG -- a PageRank-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline adapted for question answering in astronomy. The system performs token-aware chunking and per-instance, ephemeral indexing in Elasticsearch, then executes a two-stage retrieval: (i) Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to obtain a small, diverse candidate set and (ii) a reader-driven PageRank (PR) re-ranking on a similarity graph to identify a compact, mutually supportive context under a strict token budget. Our design is training-free, privacy-preserving, and reproducible, as each instance is processed through transient indexing to prevent cross-task leakage. We evaluate the pipeline on the AstroQA benchmark for astronomy QA, and demonstrate competitive performance across all difficulty levels. In particular, the RAG-enhanced Mistral-7B achieves \textbf{79.49\% accuracy} and \textbf{79.49\% F1-score}, nearly doubling the performance of its non-RAG counterpart. These results highlight the effectiveness of disciplined retrieval and refinement in boosting domain-specific reasoning, establishing a robust foundation for extending RAG to other scientific fields.

</details>


### [100] [VEOcc: Voxel-Centric Online Semantic Occupancy Prediction For Embodied Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25059)
*Ruoyu Wang,Yong Liu,Sheng Tao,Yuhang Lin,Yukai Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEOcc 是一种基于体素的在线3D占用预测与建图框架，采用递归感知-同化范式，无需预设场景尺寸，通过时空感知在线更新策略（包含跨时间逻辑聚合、可靠性置信度调制和置信度驱动的增量状态更新）实现精确高效的自主探索，在多个基准上达到SOTA，并具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的方法在结构边界保真度上存在不足，且严重依赖预定义的场景尺寸先验，限制了操作效率；需要一种无需初始尺度估计、能够高效进行开放式地图扩展的框架。

Method: 提出VEOcc体素中心框架，采用递归感知-同化范式；提出时空感知在线更新策略，包含跨时间逻辑聚合（TLA）保证时序一致性、可靠性感知置信度调制（RCM）校准空间不确定性、置信度驱动增量状态更新（CSU）实现鲁棒的全局状态同化。

Result: 在Occ-ScanNet和EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上达到新SOTA；在自采集视频序列上的零样本评估中展现了鲁棒的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: VEOcc为自主探索提供了一种精确高效的解决方案，无需场景先验即可实现开放式地图扩展，且具备良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Crucial for autonomous exploration, online 3D occupancy prediction and mapping incrementally constructs dense spatial representations on the fly. However, recent Gaussian-centric methods struggle with structural boundary fidelity and rely heavily on predefined scene-size priors, fundamentally limiting their operational efficiency. In this work, we present VEOcc, a voxel-centric framework formulated as a recursive perception-and-assimilation paradigm. By eliminating the need for initial scale estimation, VEOcc enables highly streamlined, open-ended map expansion. Furthermore, to robustly aggregate noisy temporal observations within the discrete voxel space, we propose a Spatio-Temporal-Aware Online Update Strategy. It integrates Cross-Temporal Logit Aggregation (TLA) for temporal consistency, Reliability-Aware Confidence Modulation (RCM) for spatial uncertainty calibration, and Confidence-Driven Incremental State Update (CSU) for robust global state assimilation. % Extensive experiments on Occ-ScanNet and EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet demonstrate that VEOcc establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both local and embodied settings, providing an accurate and efficient solution for real-world exploration. Extensive experiments on Occ-ScanNet and EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet demonstrate that VEOcc establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both local and embodied settings. Notably, zero-shot evaluations on self-collected video sequences further confirm its robust out-of-distribution generalization capability in completely unseen real-world environments. Ultimately, our framework provides an accurate and highly efficient solution for autonomous exploration. Code and supplementary visualizations are available on our project page: https://wryzju.github.io/VEOcc/.

</details>


### [101] [WorldCraft: From Camera Navigation to Object Manipulation in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25077)
*Bohai Gu,Taiyi Wu,Yueyang Yuan,Jian Liu,Xiaocheng Lu,Dazhao Du,Jie Zhang,Jinxiang Lai,Shuai Yang,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldCraft是一个将交互式视频世界模型从仅相机导航扩展到物体级轨迹控制的框架，通过归一化世界轨迹（NWT）、空间路径LoRA（SP-LoRA）和轨迹锚定状态持久化（TASP）三个核心组件，实现用户点击并绘制路径后，物体沿指定轨迹运动同时相机可继续导航场景的功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型仅支持相机级别的交互（视角导航），无法对单个物体进行操作，而现实世界交互本质上是物体中心的，因此现有模型更接近被动场景观察者而非可操作环境。

Method: 1) NWT：在相机不变的世界坐标系中表示用户绘制的运动轨迹，并动态重新投影到当前相机姿态下，分离物体运动与相机引起的屏幕空间位移；2) SP-LoRA：通过模型的空间控制路径注入世界空间信号，添加物体操作能力同时保留预训练的相机控制器；3) TASP：将世界轨迹视为持久空间状态，在轨迹条件生成后刷新自回归记忆，使移动物体在离开相机视野后仍能以其更新后的位置重新出现。

Result: 实验表明WorldCraft实现了精确的物体控制，在仅相机评估下保持视频世界模型的相机保真度，并在长自回归滚动（含离机暂离）中维持物体状态。

Conclusion: WorldCraft成功将视频世界模型的交互空间从相机导航扩展到物体级轨迹操作，为构建真正可操作的环境交互模型迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Recent video-based world models have made pixel-space environments interactive at the camera level: users can navigate viewpoints while the model generates coherent visual continuations. Yet their action spaces remain incomplete: users can move the camera, but cannot act on individual objects. Since real-world interaction is inherently object-centric, such models remain closer to passive scene observers than truly manipulable environments. We present WorldCraft, a framework that expands interactive video world models from camera navigation to object-level trajectory actions. Given a user click and a sketched path, WorldCraft generates future frames in which the selected object follows the prescribed trajectory while the camera continues to navigate the scene. WorldCraft achieves this through a trajectory-centric control pipeline: First, Normalized World Trajectory (NWT) represents user-drawn motion in a camera-invariant world coordinate system and dynamically re-projects it under the current camera pose, separating object motion from camera-induced screen-space displacement; Spatial-Pathway LoRA (SP-LoRA) then injects this world-space signal through the model's spatial-control pathway, adding object manipulation capability while preserving the pretrained camera controller; finally, Trajectory-Anchored State Persistence (TASP) treats the world trajectory as a persistent spatial state and refreshes autoregressive memory after trajectory-conditioned generation, allowing moved objects to reappear at their updated positions after leaving the camera view. Experiments show that WorldCraft enables accurate object control, preserves the video-based world model's camera fidelity under camera-only evaluation, and maintains object state across long autoregressive rollouts with off-camera excursions.

</details>


### [102] [Uncertainty-DTW for Sequences and Visual Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25110)
*Lei Wang,Syuan-Hao Li,Yongsheng Gao,Piotr Koniusz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性感知对齐框架uDTW，通过为每对对应关系赋予正态分布并采用最大似然估计目标进行结构化匹配，实现了对噪声鲁棒且可解释的概率对齐，在时序和视觉标记对齐任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法（如DTW及其可微变体）依赖确定性相似度量，对异质性和噪声特征敏感，缺乏对匹配不确定性的建模能力。

Method: 提出uDTW框架，将每对对应关系建模为具有异方差不确定性的正态分布，通过最大似然估计目标进行结构化路径匹配，包含精度加权匹配项（抑制不可靠特征）和对数方差正则化项（防止退化解），并扩展至视觉标记的结构化匹配。

Result: 在多个领域评估中，uDTW一致优于现有方法；学习到的不确定性能够反映语义重要性，低不确定性对应语义相关区域，高不确定性对应模糊/噪声区域。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知对齐是一种通用、鲁棒且可解释的结构化数据学习框架，建立了对齐、注意力与不确定性建模之间的关联。

Abstract: Aligning structured data is a fundamental problem in computer vision and machine learning, underlying tasks such as time series analysis, human action recognition, and visual representation learning. Existing alignment methods, including Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) and its differentiable variants, rely on deterministic similarity measures and are therefore sensitive to heterogeneous and noisy features. In this work, we introduce uncertainty-aware alignment, a probabilistic framework that models pairwise correspondences with heteroscedastic uncertainty and performs structured matching along alignment paths. Our formulation, uncertainty-DTW (uDTW), assigns each correspondence a Normal distribution and parametrizes each alignment path by a Maximum Likelihood Estimate objective consisting of (i) a precision-weighted matching term that suppresses unreliable features, and (ii) a log-variance regularization that prevents degenerate solutions. This yields a probabilistic alignment mechanism that is robust to noise and interpretable, as uncertainty directly reflects the reliability of matches. We further generalize this framework from temporal sequences to tokenized visual representations, enabling structured matching over sets of visual tokens. The learned uncertainty can be interpreted as a reverse-attention: semantically relevant regions exhibit low uncertainty and dominate the alignment, while ambiguous/noisy regions have high uncertainty. This provides a connection between alignment, attention, and uncertainty modeling. We evaluate the proposed framework across diverse domains. The results demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods and show that learned uncertainty correlates with semantic importance. These findings establish uncertainty-aware alignment as a general, robust, and interpretable framework for learning from structured data.

</details>


### [103] [Trust-Aware Joint Feature-Prediction Discrepancy for Robust Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25119)
*Xi Ding,Lei Wang,Syuan-Hao Li,Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出信任感知领域自适应框架，通过联合特征-预测差异（JFPD）来建模领域差异，并引入不确定性感知信任和语义对齐信任两种机制对样本进行可靠性加权，从而在领域自适应中优先对齐可信区域，实验表明该方法优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域自适应方法仅从特征空间或预测空间单一角度估计领域差异，忽略了分布偏移下特征表示和语义预测都可能不可靠的问题，对所有目标样本一视同仁会导致误导性对齐和次优迁移。

Method: 提出联合特征-预测差异（JFPD）统一框架，同时建模表征散度和预测散度，并通过两种信任机制加权：不确定性感知信任（基于预测熵）和语义对齐信任（基于特征空间中原型相似度），优先对齐置信且语义一致的样本。

Result: 在标准基准上一致取得优于现有方法的自适应性能，且JFPD估计的领域差异与目标域误差具有良好的相关性。

Conclusion: 首次在领域自适应中建模特征与预测交互的信任重要性，提供了一种可靠性感知的领域差异估计方法。

Abstract: Domain adaptation aims to mitigate performance degradation caused by distribution shifts between a labeled source domain and an unlabeled or sparsely labeled target domain. Most existing approaches estimate domain discrepancy either in feature space or in prediction space. However, these single-perspective strategies overlook a critical problem under domain shift: the reliability of the signals used for alignment. In practice, both learned representations and semantic predictions may become unreliable, and treating all target samples equally can lead to misleading alignment and suboptimal transfer. We introduce trust-aware domain adaptation, a principled framework that models domain discrepancy through the reliability of feature and prediction signals. Central to our approach is the Joint Feature-Prediction Discrepancy (JFPD), a unified formulation that jointly captures representation divergence and prediction divergence while weighting their contributions by sample-specific trust. Trust is quantified via two complementary mechanisms: uncertainty-aware trust, derived from prediction entropy to suppress unreliable predictions, and semantic-alignment trust, computed from prototype similarity in feature space to emphasize well-aligned representations. By prioritizing confident and semantically consistent samples while down-weighting noisy or ambiguous ones, JFPD provides a reliability-aware estimate of domain discrepancy. We further integrate JFPD into a training objective that guides adaptation toward trustworthy regions of the target domain. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves superior adaptation performance and yields discrepancy estimates that correlate with target-domain error. This work addresses, for the first time, the importance of modeling trust in the interaction between features and predictions for domain adaptation.

</details>


### [104] [Methodology for Creating a Clinically Verified Dermoscopic Image Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25168)
*Kozachok Elena Sergeevna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套移动皮肤镜影像数据集的构建方法论，包括标准化采集流程、16字段结构化元数据模型和多阶段专家诊断验证，最终构建了包含1,026张图像、涵盖9种疾病类别、所有39例恶性病变均经组织学验证的临床验证数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有国际皮肤镜数据集多来自与俄罗斯常规门诊及移动皮肤镜实践差异显著的环境，且自动化诊断系统的性能不仅依赖图像数量，更取决于采集流程的可重复性、元数据的完整性和诊断标签的可靠性，因此需要一套适应俄罗斯临床实际情况的标准化数据集构建方法。

Method: 方法整合三个核心组件：(1) 移动皮肤镜图像采集标准操作规程(SOP)；(2) 包含16个结构化元数据字段、按6个临床模块组织、兼容ISIC标注的信息模型；(3) 多阶段专家验证流程（初始临床标注→三位专家共识审查→所有恶性病变组织学确认）。

Result: 2025年6月至2026年5月间收集443例患者共1,026张皮肤镜图像（排除18张重复），涵盖9种疾病类别，39例恶性病变（18例黑色素瘤、15例基底细胞癌、6例鳞状细胞癌）均经组织学验证。患者年龄2-90岁（中位38岁），女性279例（63%），男性164例（37%）。每张图像附有专家标注的皮肤镜结构和明确的验证阶段字段。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为适用于独立模型评估、领域偏移分析、可解释性研究及后续扩展的临床验证试点资源。

Abstract: This study presents a methodology for constructing a clinically verified dataset of dermatoscopic images for medical informatics research. The relevance of the work is driven by the fact that the performance of automated diagnostic support systems depends not only on the volume of images, but also on the reproducibility of the image acquisition procedure, the completeness of structured metadata, and the reliability of diagnostic labels. International collections were primarily created under conditions that differ substantially from routine Russian outpatient practice and mobile dermatoscopy. The proposed methodology integrates three interconnected components: (1) a standard operating procedure (SOP) for acquiring images via mobile dermatoscopy, (2) an information model comprising 16 structured metadata fields organized into six clinically oriented blocks in ISIC-compatible notation, and (3) a multi-stage expert verification of diagnostic labels (initial clinical annotation, consensus review by three specialists, and histological confirmation of all malignant neoplasms). Using this methodology, a dataset of 1,026 unique dermatoscopic images from 443 patients was collected between June 2025 and May 2026. From 1,044 initial records, 18 duplicates were excluded. The dataset includes nine nosological categories; all 39 malignant lesions (18 melanomas, 15 basal cell carcinomas, and 6 squamous cell carcinomas) were histologically verified. Patient age ranged from 2 to 90 years (median 38), with 279 females (63%) and 164 males (37%). Each image is accompanied by expert-annotated dermatoscopic structures and an explicit verification_stage field indicating the level of diagnostic confirmation. The resulting dataset serves as a pilot clinically verified resource suitable for independent model evaluation, domain shift analysis, interpretability studies, and further expansion.

</details>


### [105] [Discrepancy Minimization Improves Cross-Hospital Robustness in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25175)
*Ben Vardi,Dana Schonberger,Yuval Friedmann,Zohar Yakhini,Iris Barshack,Alexander Loebel,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用局部最大均值差异（LMMD）目标微调病理基础模型（PFMs），以提升其在不同医院间的鲁棒性，覆盖域适应（有目标域无标签数据）和域泛化（无目标域数据）两种场景，在补丁和切片级别均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型在医院间迁移时性能下降严重（领域漂移问题），亟需提升其跨域鲁棒性。

Method: 通过局部最大均值差异（LMMD）目标微调PFMs，LMMD同时适用于域适应（利用无标签目标域数据）和域泛化（完全无目标域数据）两种设置。

Result: 在补丁级和切片级实验均显示，该方法在多种PFMs和多个任务上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: LMMD微调是提升病理基础模型跨医院泛化能力的有效方法，且同时适用于域适应和域泛化场景。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) have advanced rapidly in recent years and support training classifiers for a range of histopathology tasks. However, their robustness across hospitals remains limited: performance often degrades when training a classifier on data from one hospital and evaluating it on another target hospital. We address this challenge by fine-tuning PFMs with a local maximum mean discrepancy (LMMD) objective that applies to two settings: domain adaptation, where unlabeled target-hospital data is available, and domain generalization, where target-hospital data is unavailable at all. Experiments at both the patch- and slide-level show consistent improvements across multiple PFMs and tasks.

</details>


### [106] [Injecting Image Guidance into Text-Conditioned Diffusion Models at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25191)
*Agata Żywot,Iason Skylitsis,Thijmen Nijdam,Zoe Tzifa-Kratira,Derck Prinzhorn,Konrad Szewczyk,Aritra Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VCF是一种无需重新训练即可在推理时同时接受图像和文本双重条件控制的扩散模型方法，通过轻量级对齐器将CLIP图像特征映射到文本嵌入空间，实现视觉概念注入，在保持文本对齐的同时成功迁移参考图像的风格、构图和配色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型（如Stable Diffusion）无法在推理时注入视觉引导（如草图、风格），现有方法要么需要昂贵的微调，要么依赖风格迁移技术导致语义与文本提示不对齐。

Method: VCF包含三个组件：(1)轻量级对齐器，使用InfoNCE和交叉注意力重建损失将图像tokens映射到文本嵌入流形；(2)融合策略，保留文本和视觉语义；(3)可选的提示-噪声优化模块用于测试时精细调整。

Result: 成功迁移参考图像的风格、构图和配色等视觉属性，同时保持文本提示对齐。在文本对齐（CLIP分数）和视觉对应（LPIPS）之间存在权衡，VCF在参考保真度上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: VCF是首个在推理时无需概念特定训练即可实现图像和文本双重条件控制的方法，在视觉属性迁移和提示遵循之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models like Stable Diffusion generate high-quality images from text, but lack a way to inject visual guidance (e.g. sketches, styles) at inference without retraining. Existing methods either require computationally expensive fine-tuning or rely on style transfer techniques that risk semantic misalignment with textual prompts. We introduce Visual Concept Fusion (VCF), the first method offering dual conditioning on both an image and text prompt at inference time without any concept-specific training. VCF enables visual concept injection into Stable Diffusion by aligning CLIP image features with the text embedding space. VCF consists of three components: (1) a lightweight aligner that maps image tokens to the text embedding manifold using InfoNCE and cross-attention reconstruction losses, (2) a fusion strategy that preserves both textual and visual semantics, and (3) an optional Prompt-Noise Optimization (PNO) module for test-time refinement. Our experiments demonstrate that VCF successfully transfers visual attributes including style, composition, and color palette from reference images while maintaining prompt adherence. Quantitative results show a trade-off between text alignment (CLIP score) and visual correspondence (LPIPS), with VCF outperforming baselines in reference fidelity.

</details>


### [107] [SpongeBob: Sync-Aware Harmonious Audio-Visual Generative Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25193)
*Sen Liang,Cong Wang,Fengbin Guan,Zhentao Yu,Yiting Lu,Yuanzhi Wang,Yuan Zhou,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpongeBob，首个端到端音视频联合编辑框架，通过双向跨模态交互机制解决音视频不同步和语义冲突问题，在同步性和上下文一致性指标上分别提升30%和12.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法采用解耦流水线，缺乏双向模态交互，导致音视频不同步以及生成音频与保留内容之间的上下文冲突。

Method: 提出SpongeBob框架，包含Sync-Aware Mechanism（双向注意力、时间对齐、空间约束实现同步）和Context-Aware Module（声学与视觉上下文注意力防止语义冲突），以及Sync-Preserving Training and Guidance (SPTG)提升对齐质量，并构建了大规模数据集和评测基准。

Result: Sync-C指标提升30%，Ctx-F1指标提升12.5%，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: SpongeBob作为首个端到端音视频联合编辑框架，通过双向跨模态交互有效解决了同步与上下文一致性问题，为音视频联合编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Visual and acoustic events in the physical world are inherently coupled, yet existing video editing methods typically adopt decoupled pipelines, lacking bidirectional modality interaction. This results in two key limitations: (i) audio-visual desynchronization and (ii) contextual conflicts between generated audio and preserved content. To address these, we propose SpongeBob, the first end-to-end audio-visual joint editing framework featuring bidirectional cross-modal interaction. For synchronization, a Sync-Aware Mechanism aligns visual edits with sound events via bidirectional attention, temporal alignment, and spatial constraints. For contextual consistency, a Context-Aware Module leverages acoustic and visual context attention to prevent semantic clashes. Additionally, we introduce Sync-Preserving Training and Guidance (SPTG) to enhance alignment without degrading quality. Due to the scarcity of paired data, we construct a scalable data pipeline and a large-scale subject-level dataset. We also propose SpongeBob-Bench for systematic evaluation. Experiments show SpongeBob significantly outperforms existing baselines, improving Sync-C by 30% and Ctx-F1 by 12.5%. Our project page is available at: https://hy-spongebob.github.io/.

</details>


### [108] [Baton: Explicit Semantic Blueprints for Joint Video-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25195)
*Shuyuan Tu,Qi Tian,Zihan Yang,Yue Wu,Xintong Han,Weijie Kong,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jian-Wei Zhang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Baton提出了首个引入显式语义规划到联合视频-音频生成的框架，通过VA-Planner生成语义对齐的规划token作为蓝图，并设计了相对语义RoPE实现空间-时间对齐，解决了现有扩散模型在音视频生成中缺乏长期规划和细粒度语义对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源扩散模型依赖粗粒度文本嵌入引导音视频去噪，丢失了细粒度语义信息，且缺乏共享的长程规划，导致去噪轨迹不协调、跨模态对齐脆弱。

Method: 1) VA-Planner：多模态语言模型配备双语义对齐塔，通过可学习查询与音视频特征交叉注意力，生成语义对齐的音视频规划token作为关键帧级蓝图；2) 将规划token通过交叉注意力层注入扩散骨干，补充粗粒度文本嵌入；3) 相对语义RoPE：将规划token与扩散隐变量映射到共享时空坐标系，实现位置感知的语义注意力。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，Baton在定性和定量指标上均取得了有效提升。

Conclusion: 显式语义规划能够有效协调音视频去噪轨迹，提升跨模态对齐质量和细粒度语义保留能力，为联合音视频生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current open-source diffusion models struggle to generate stable and synchronized audio-visual content, particularly in scenarios demanding complex semantic reasoning. The root cause is that existing methods rely on coarse text embeddings from off-the-shelf encoders to guide audio-video denoising, which discards fine-grained semantics and, critically, lacks a shared long-horizon plan, leading to uncoordinated denoising trajectories and fragile cross-modal alignment. We propose Baton, the first framework that introduces explicit semantic planning into joint video-audio generation. Our key insight is that complementing coarse text guidance with semantically rich, modality-aware planned tokens, jointly reasoned and mutually aligned before denoising, can simultaneously restore fine-grained semantic detail and establish a shared blueprint that coordinates both audio and video denoising trajectories. Concretely, Baton first introduces the VA-Planner, a multimodal language model equipped with dual semantic alignment towers, where learnable queries cross-attend to both video and audio features to produce a pair of semantically aligned video and audio planned tokens as keyframe-level blueprints.
