This mechanism first anchors reasoning paths via scaffold-augmented supervision to mitigate the learning complexity and distribution drift of direct SFT on raw data, then leverages RL to strip the scaffold template, thereby effectively transitioning the reasoning paths into intrinsic model capabilities. Experimental results on multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that our method substantially advances the model performance frontier, improving the base model by 10.3\% across eight multimodal benchmarks and achieving state-of-the-art results. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [144] [Mosaic: Compositional Multi-Concept Erasure via Vector Field Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25574)
*Junseok Ko,Jungwoo Kim,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合多概念擦除任务，用于在流式文本到图像模型中同时移除场景中的多个目标概念，并提出了评估基准CoME-Bench和新型框架Mosaic。Mosaic通过动态构建概念特定掩码并选择性融合，在不需额外优化的情况下有效移除多个目标概念，同时保留非目标上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除研究通常假设每张图像只有一个目标概念，但现代流式T2I模型能生成包含多个概念的复杂场景，现有方法无法处理这种组合多概念擦除需求。

Method: 提出了Mosaic框架，利用向量场中目标概念的空间局部性，动态构建概念特定掩码并选择性融合，无需额外优化即可实现多概念擦除。

Result: Mosaic在复杂组合场景中有效移除多个目标概念，同时保留非目标上下文。

Conclusion: Mosaic框架成功解决了流式T2I模型中的组合多概念擦除问题，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a key research direction for ensuring safe and ethical image synthesis in Text-to-Image (T2I) models. While existing studies have explored concept erasure across multiple concepts, they typically assume only a single target concept per image, a limitation increasingly exposed by modern flow-based T2I models, which can generate complex scenes with multiple concepts simultaneously. To address this gap, we introduce compositional multi-concept erasure, a new task that aims to simultaneously remove multiple target concepts within a single scene. We propose CoME-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating compositional multi-concept erasure, which covers both intra- and cross-category scenarios. We further propose Mosaic, a novel framework for multi-concept erasure in flow-based T2I models, which exploits the spatial locality of target concepts in the vector field by dynamically constructing concept-specific masks and selectively blending them without additional optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Mosaic effectively removes multiple target concepts in complex compositional scenes while preserving non-target contexts.

</details>


### [145] [Artifact Correction for Echo-Planar Imaging at Low-Field and Ultra-Low-Field MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25589)
*Sisi Qiao,Yilin Yu,Tiecheng Lin,Yuhao Liu,Jiajia Sun,Xiaoling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考扫描的峰对齐与插值重采样相结合的EPI伪影校正方法，有效抑制了低场和超低场MRI中的Nyquist鬼影伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低场和超低场MRI中的EPI成像因奇偶k空间错位而产生严重的Nyquist鬼影伪影，传统参考扫描法依赖额外数据且校正效果有限，亟需一种无需参考数据的实用校正方案。

Method: 首先采用基于峰对齐的鬼影校正方法在无参考数据条件下校正奇偶行位移，然后通过插值与重采样策略进一步抑制残余伪影，形成完整校正管线。

Result: 该方法有效减轻了Nyquist鬼影，改善了结构连续性和信号均匀性；峰对齐法单独即可达到与参考扫描法相当的校正效果，插值重采样进一步抑制残余伪影，实现超低场条件下的可靠脑结构可视化。

Conclusion: 本文提出的无参考校正管线为低场和超低场EPI提供了高效鬼影抑制方案，扩展了低场MRI的临床适用性，并为超低场EPI-DWI成像提供了理论指导和实践经验。

Abstract: Purpose: Echo-planar imaging (EPI) in low-field (LF) and ultra-low-field MRI (ULF) suffers from severe Nyquist ghost artifacts due to odd-even k-space misalignment. This study develops a reference-free artifact correction pipeline that reduces reliance on conventional reference scans while achieving improved ghost suppression. Methods: Starting from the traditional reference-scan-based ghost artifact correction method, we first introduce a peak-alignment-based ghost artifact correction method to correct odd-even line displacement without reference data. To further reduce residual artifacts, an interpolation-and-resampling strategy is applied. The combined method was evaluated using EPI and diffusion-weighted EPI data in LF and ULF. Results: The proposed pipeline effectively mitigated Nyquist ghosts, improved structural continuity, and enhanced signal uniformity. Peak-alignment-based ghost artifact correction method alone provided comparable artifact suppression to reference-scan-based ghost artifact correction method, while interpolation and resampling further suppressed residual artifacts, enabling reliable visualization of brain structures under ULF conditions. Conclusion: A practical, reference-free correction pipeline is presented for LF and ULF EPI, combining peak-alignment-based ghost artifact correction method and interpolation-resampling to achieve efficient ghost suppression and expand the clinical applicability of low-field MRI systems, providing both theoretical guidance and practical experience for ULF EPI-based DWI imaging.

</details>


### [146] [How Far Has AI Come in Liver Fibrosis Staging? A Large-Scale Real-World Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25595)
*Yuanye Liu,Nannan Shi,Zhejia Zhang,Hanxiao Zhang,Boya Wang,Derong Yu,Nao Wang,Yuxin Jin,Yang Zhou,Kunhao Yuan,Siqi Wang,Lida Yang,Xu Qiao,Wentao Liu,Xuelei He,Xin Hong,Guoyan Zheng,Xin Chen,Guang-Zhong Yang,Le Zhang,Lei Li,Yuxin Shi,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍LiFS——基于MICCAI 2025 CARE-Liver挑战赛构建的大规模多中心肝纤维化分期基准数据集（610例患者，多序列MRI），系统评估了9种独立AI方法在真实临床异质性条件下的表现。结果表明：最优AI方法与资深放射科医生相当，中位AI表现接近初级放射科医生；跨中心异质性、标签不平衡和对比增强序列变异性是主要挑战；方法论设计（空间配准、输入维度、多模态融合策略、骨干架构）影响跨中心鲁棒性，但单一选择无法弥补差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质性多中心条件下系统评估AI在肝纤维化分期中的实际临床能力，填补该领域缺乏大规模真实世界基准的空白。

Method: 构建LiFS数据集（610名患者，多中心、多扫描仪、多序列MRI，钆塞酸增强序列，组织病理学确认标注），从96个注册团队中筛选9种独立方法，与同队列放射科医生结果进行系统比较。

Result: 最佳AI与资深放射科医生相当，某些场景显著超越初级医生；中位AI接近初级医生水平；跨中心异质性、标签不平衡和对比增强序列变异性是主要障碍；空间配准、输入维度、多模态融合策略和骨干架构设计影响鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LiFS提供了严格的真实世界基准，揭示了当前AI在肝纤维化分期中达到临床级部署的关键挑战（跨中心鲁棒性不足），为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Despite years of methodological progress, how far AI has come in liver fibrosis staging has never been systematically evaluated under the heterogeneous, multi-center conditions that define clinical practice. To address this gap, we introduce LiFS, a large-scale dataset and benchmark derived from the MICCAI 2025 CARE-Liver challenge, comprising 610 patients across multiple centers and scanners with multi-sequence MRI. To the best of our knowledge, LiFS is the first benchmark providing complete gadoxetic acid-enhanced sequences with histopathology-confirmed annotations from diverse real-world scanners. Through systematic evaluation of 9 independently developed methods selected from 96 registered teams against in-cohort radiologist reference results, our findings address how far current AI has progressed toward clinical-level liver fibrosis staging from three complementary perspectives. First, against radiologists, the best AI methods were broadly comparable to the senior radiologist and significantly exceeded the junior radiologist in selected settings, while median AI performance generally approached junior-radiologist levels. Second, from a data perspective, cross-center heterogeneity, label imbalance, and contrast-enhanced sequence variability emerge as the dominant challenges for AI methods. Third, from a technical perspective, methodological design choices, including spatial registration, input dimensionality, multi-modal fusion strategy, and backbone architecture, appear to modulate cross-center robustness, although no single choice alone closes the gap. Overall, LiFS provides a rigorous real-world benchmark for positioning the current state of AI in liver fibrosis staging and for enabling future research on the key challenges that limit clinically reliable deployment.

</details>


### [147] [UAV-OVO: Out-of-Viewpoint Generalization in UAV Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25615)
*Yu Xia,Zhengbo Zhang,Shuaihu Zhang,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UAV-OVO基准，专门用于无人机动作识别的视角泛化评估，揭示了低俯视角训练到高俯视角测试时的显著性能下降；并提出LATER方法，通过LoRA锚定测试时特征重中心化，减少视角偏移带来的语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机动作识别基准忽略了部署阶段存在的视角偏移问题——训练数据来自低俯视角，测试时却可能遇到高俯视角，导致模型依赖视角特定的捷径而泛化失败。

Method: 1) UAV-OVO：从未标定视频计算视角分数，利用视角隔离带将低俯视角视频分为训练集和分布内测试集，高俯视角视频作为分布外测试集，并确保ID/OOD测试集类别分布匹配。2) LATER：先使用LoRA适配识别器，再将学到的LoRA子空间作为语义锚点，将目标域位移投影到LoRA子空间的正交补空间后再进行特征重中心化。

Result: 在多种代表性视频识别器上，UAV-OVO揭示了显著的ID/OOD性能差距：在低俯视角训练集上拟合良好的模型，难以泛化到高俯视角测试集，暴露了聚合准确率下隐藏的视角捷径。

Conclusion: UAV-OVO与LATER共同提供了可控的测试平台和实用的自适应方法，用于视角鲁棒的无人机视频理解。

Abstract: UAV action recognition faces a deployment shift that standard benchmarks often obscure: a model trained on UAV footage captured from low-depression viewpoints may be required to recognize the same action classes from high-depression viewpoints. While the action labels remain unchanged, this shift alters body visibility, motion projection, and scene context, encouraging models to rely on viewpoint-specific shortcuts. We introduce UAV-OVO, an Out-of-Viewpoint generalization benchmark for UAV action recognition. UAV-OVO derives view scores from uncalibrated videos, uses a view-isolation band to assign low-depression videos to the training and in-distribution test splits while reserving high-depression videos for out-of-distribution testing, and constructs ID/OOD test sets matched by class distribution so that performance differences reflect viewpoint shift rather than label imbalance. Across representative video recognizers, UAV-OVO reveals a substantial ID/OOD gap: models that fit the low-depression training distribution well often fail to transfer to held-out high-depression views, exposing viewpoint shortcuts hidden by aggregate accuracy. We further propose LATER, LoRA-Anchored Test-time Re-centering, which first adapts the recognizer with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and then uses the learned LoRA subspace as a semantic anchor for online feature re-centering. Specifically, LATER projects target-domain displacement onto the orthogonal complement of the LoRA subspace before re-centering features, reducing viewpoint-induced drift while preserving task-relevant semantics. Together, UAV-OVO and LATER provide a controlled testbed and a practical adaptation method for viewpoint-robust UAV video understanding.

</details>


### [148] [StreamOV: Streaming Omni-Video Understanding via Evidence-Guided Memory and Response Triggering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25621)
*Ming Xie,Zizheng Huang,Xudong Tan,Chao Wang,Xiangyu Zeng,Wenxiao Wu,Tao Chen,Limin Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamOV提出了一种面向流式全模态视频理解的高效在线框架，通过多模态证据引导的长短时记忆和隐状态触发响应机制，解决了现有方法无法处理连续音视频流和缺乏在线交互能力的问题，并在首个在线多轮全模态评测基准SOVBench上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态方法设计上仅针对离线场景，存在两个根本缺陷：(1) 缺乏对不断增长的音视频长期上下文的有效管理机制，无法自主选择合适时机发起响应；(2) 现有基准局限于离线单轮问答，无法评估连续多轮的流式交互场景。因此需要构建适用于流式在线音视频推理的统一框架。

Method: StreamOV包含两个核心模块：(1) 多模态证据引导的长短时记忆（Multimodal Evidence-Guided Long-Short Term Memory），在固定预算下将历史音视频上下文压缩为紧凑的信息性证据；(2) 隐状态驱动触发机制（Hidden-State-Driven Trigger），利用隐状态决定何时响应，避免显式的静默令牌生成和外部路由器的使用。

Result: 在多个流式视频和全模态视频基准上取得SOTA性能，同时在在线和离线视频理解场景中均展现出有效性。

Conclusion: StreamOV成功弥合了全模态方法与流式应用场景之间的鸿沟，为在线音视频推理提供了高效的解决方案，SOVBench也为该领域提供了首个全面的在线多轮评估基准。

Abstract: While streaming omni-video understanding demands continuous perception and proactive, real-time interaction, this crucial area remains largely under-explored. Current omni-modal methods are inherently designed for offline settings, limiting their applicability in streaming scenarios due to two fundamental flaws. First, they lack robust mechanisms to manage continuously growing audio-visual context over long horizons and cannot autonomously initiate responses at opportune moments. Second, existing benchmarks are predominantly confined to offline, single-turn question answering, failing to capture continuous, multi-turn streaming interactions. To bridge these gaps, we propose StreamOV, a novel Streaming Omni-Video understanding framework for efficient online audio-visual reasoning with bounded memory and proactive response triggering. Specifically, StreamOV introduces a multimodal evidence-guided long-short term memory that condenses historical audio-visual context into compact informative evidence under a fixed budget. It further employs a hidden-state-driven trigger to decide when to respond, avoiding explicit silence-token generation and external routers. We also curate SOVBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for online, multi-turn omni-modal evaluation. Extensive experiments show that StreamOV achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse streaming and omni-video benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness for both online and offline video understanding.

</details>


### [149] [Hierarchical Consistency Learning for Test-time Adaptation in Camouflage Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25651)
*Mingfeng Zha,Tianyu Li,Guoqing Wang,Yunqiang Pei,Chaofan Qiao,Jiening Zhang,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次一致性学习(HCL)框架，通过测试时自适应和层次表示重建、任务亲和引导、原型一致性校准等模块，在伪装目标检测中实现动态表示再校准，在多个数据集和退化设置下均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD方法受限于静态训练-冻结范式，存在域刚性和标注依赖性问题，限制了其对场景变化和未见伪装模式的适应性。

Method: 提出HCL框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 层次表示重建(HRR)：协同空间重建与双流频域分解，缓解特征纠缠；2) 任务亲和引导(TAG)：通过通道级亲和力跨分支传播知识，对齐局部判别线索；3) 原型一致性校准(PCC)：聚合区域特征为紧凑原型并建立原型-特征相似度，施加隐式层次约束。

Result: 在四个伪装目标和四个水下目标数据集上，在三种退化设置下，该方法一致优于现有最先进方法，展现出在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过测试时自适应和层次一致性学习，有效克服了COD中域刚性和标注依赖问题，提升了模型对场景变化和未知伪装模式的适应能力。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to localize targets that exhibit minimal perceptual differences from backgrounds through physical attributes. Existing methods, constrained by the static train-then-freeze paradigm, suffer from domain rigidity and annotation dependency, limiting their adaptability to scene variations and unseen camouflage patterns. To overcome these, we propose the hierarchical consistency learning (HCL) framework, which integrates test-time adaptation for dynamic representation recalibration. Specifically, we design the hierarchical representation reconstruction (HRR) to alleviate feature entanglement by synergizing spatial reconstruction with dual-stream frequency-domain decomposition, enhancing robustness against appearance homogenization. The pixel and spectrum inference provide structural and contextual priors. We further introduce task affinity guidance (TAG) to propagate knowledge across branches via channel-wise affinity, aligning local discriminative cues and mitigating semantic drift. To ensure semantic invariance, we formulate the prototype consistency calibration (PCC), which aggregates region features into compact prototypes and establishes prototype-feature similarity. This imposes implicit and hierarchical constraints that bridge task and representation gaps. Extensive experiments across four camouflaged and four underwater object benchmarks, under three degradation settings, demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, highlighting its robustness and generalization under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [150] [ARMA-C3: A Contrastive ARMA Convolutional Framework for Unsupervised and Semi-supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25657)
*VSS Tejaswi Abburi,Saurabh J. Shigwan,Nitin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMA-C3是一个统一的基于对比学习和图割正则化的无监督/半监督图学习框架，通过在五个生物医学数据集上的节点分类实验，在标签稀缺和类别不平衡条件下取得了优于传统方法和现有图深度学习模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学和神经退行性疾病中，标注数据稀缺且成像模式复杂，导致早期准确诊断困难。传统机器学习方法忽略了样本间的相互关系，无法捕捉受试者级别的依赖信息。

Method: 提出ARMA-C3框架，将样本/图像建模为图节点，利用对比学习（contrastive learning）和图割正则化（graph-cut regularization）学习结构上有意义且具有判别性的表示，支持无监督和半监督两种学习范式。

Result: 在ADNI、NIFD以及三个医学影像基准数据集上的二分类实验中，ARMA-C3在多种评估设置下优于经典聚类方法、SOTA机器学习模型和现有基于图的深度学习方法，尤其在标注有限和类别严重不平衡场景下表现突出，并展现出跨模态泛化能力。

Conclusion: ARMA-C3框架有效解决了生物医学影像中标签稀缺和类不平衡问题，通过图结构建模样本间关系提升了表示学习质量，具有良好的鲁棒性和跨模态迁移能力。

Abstract: In biomedical and neurodegenerative disorders, accurate and early disease identification remains challenging due to the scarcity of labeled data and the complexity of imaging patterns. To address these challenges, we introduce ARMA-C3, a unified unsupervised and semi-supervised graph learning framework for node classification based on contrastive learning and graph-cut regularization to learn structurally meaningful and discriminative representations. By modeling samples or images as graph nodes and exploiting inter-sample relationships, the proposed framework captures subject-level dependencies that conventional machine learning methods typically overlook. We conduct extensive binary classification experiments across five clinically relevant datasets: the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), the Neuroimaging in Frontotemporal Dementia (NIFD) dataset, and three medical imaging benchmarks (BreastMNIST, PneumoniaMNIST, and a liver ultrasound dataset). Experimental results demonstrate that ARMA-C3 achieves competitive and frequently superior performance compared to classical clustering techniques, state-of-the-art machine learning models, and existing graph-based deep learning approaches across multiple evaluation settings, particularly under limited supervision and severe class imbalance. The proposed framework further demonstrates robust representation learning and strong cross-modal generalization across diverse biomedical imaging modalities.

</details>


### [151] [StreamChar: Long-Horizon Streaming Character Audio-Video Generation with Decoupled Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25659)
*Linrui Tian,Qi Wang,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamChar提出了一种面向实时流式角色动画的联合音视频生成框架，通过LLM编排器与DiT去噪器的分离设计、两阶段蒸馏流水线以及进度感知指针和汇合块记忆机制，在单卡H100上实现实时推理，并在文本忠实度、音画同步、视觉质量和流式稳定性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时流式联合音视频生成需要满足三点：1) 生成器需按文本说话；2) 跨数据块保持视觉一致性；3) 在严格的播放预算内运行。现有方法难以同时满足这些要求——逐块自回归生成会累积文本-音频错位和视觉漂移，而低延迟所需的少步蒸馏常导致空间多样性和时间质量下降。

Method: 将长程编排与短窗音视频去噪分离：LLM编排器利用文本和历史上下文生成帧对齐的音频条件，联合音视频DiT在局部窗口内进行双向去噪（带参考帧和运动帧条件）。部署时采用两阶段蒸馏：先压缩采样器，再在在线块推演下微调学生模型。引入进度感知指针对齐生成音频与部分文本，使用汇合块记忆提供持久视觉锚点以减少长程漂移。

Result: 在短片段和长时程协议上的实验表明，StreamChar在单卡H100上实时运行，在文本忠实度、音画同步、视觉质量和流式稳定性方面均优于近期联合生成和音频驱动基线方法。

Conclusion: StreamChar通过编排-去噪分离架构和针对流式场景的蒸馏、对齐与记忆机制，有效解决了实时流式角色动画中文本对齐、视觉一致性和低延迟三者之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Real-time streaming joint audio-video generation for character animation requires a generator to speak the requested transcript, maintain visual identity across chunks, and run within a strict playback budget. These requirements are difficult to satisfy simultaneously: chunk-wise autoregressive generation can accumulate transcript-audio misalignment and visual drift, while the few-step distillation needed for low latency often degrades spatial diversity and temporal quality. We present StreamChar, a streaming framework that separates long-horizon orchestration from short-window audio-video denoising. An LLM-based orchestrator uses the transcript and historical context to produce frame-aligned audio conditions, and a joint audio-video DiT performs local bidirectional denoising with reference and motion-frame conditioning. For efficient deployment, we use a two-stage distillation pipeline that first compresses the sampler and then fine-tunes the student under online chunk rollouts. A progress-aware pointer aligns partial transcripts with generated audio during rollout training, and a sink-chunk memory provides a persistent visual anchor for reducing long-horizon drift. Experiments on short-clip and long-horizon protocols show that StreamChar runs in real time on a single H100 GPU and provides a favorable system-level trade-off among transcript fidelity, audio-visual synchronization, visual quality, and streaming stability compared with recent joint and audio-driven baselines.

</details>


### [152] [DRM: Diffusion-based Reward Model With Step-wise Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25661)
*Jaxon Zhang,Binxin Yang,Hubery Yin,Chen Li,Jing Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型的奖励模型（DRM），利用预训练扩散模型作为评估骨干，通过步级GRPO算法和步级采样策略，实现扩散模型与人类偏好的有效对齐，显著提升生成图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于VLM的奖励模型在语义对齐方面表现良好，但难以捕捉美学、构图、视觉和谐等关键感知质量属性，导致扩散模型生成结果在这些方面不尽如人意。

Method: 提出Diffusion-based Reward Model (DRM)，利用预训练扩散模型作为评估骨干；提出Step-wise GRPO算法，提供密集的逐步奖励以解决GRPO中的信用分配问题；提出Step-wise Sampling推理策略，在每一步动态评估多个生成路径以引导生成更高质量的结果。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法显著提升了生成图像的最终质量。

Conclusion: DRM利用扩散模型自身的评估能力，通过步级奖励和步级采样，有效解决了现有奖励模型在感知质量评估上的不足，实现了更稳定、更有效的对齐。

Abstract: Current mainstream methods of aligning diffusion models with human preferences typically employ VLM-based reward models. However, these reward models, pre-trained for semantic alignment, struggle to capture the essential perceptual qualities-such as aesthetics, composition, and visual harmony. In this work, we argue that a model capable of high-fidelity generation must possess a profound understanding of these visual attributes. Based on this insight, we introduce the Diffusion-based Reward Model (DRM), a novel paradigm that use the pre-trained diffusion model as a powerful evaluative backbone. A key advantage of the DRM is its unique ability to assess not only the final image but also the noisy intermediate latents at any stage of the generative process. We leverage this step-wise evaluative capacity in two ways. First, we propose Step-wise GRPO, a reinforcement learning algorithm that provides dense, per-step rewards to resolve the imprecise credit assignment problem in GRPO algorithm, leading to more stable and effective alignment. Second, we introduce Step-wise Sampling, a novel inference strategy that employs the DRM as a dynamic guide to evaluate multiple generation paths at each step, steering the process towards higher-quality outcomes. Extensive experiments confirm that our approach significantly enhances the final quality of generated images. Code: https://github.com/jjaxonx/DRM.

</details>


### [153] [Towards Open-World Referring Expression Comprehension: A Benchmark with Training-free Multi-task Consistency Checker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25706)
*Zongjian Wu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenRef基准，用于复杂场景下的指代表达理解（REC），涵盖多样视觉场景、多/零目标样本及丰富词汇类型，并引入新指标N3R和训练增强策略MCC，显著提升现有REC模型在开放世界中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REC基准存在场景简单、假设每个表达式唯一对应一个目标等局限，阻碍了REC模型在开放世界环境中的部署。

Method: 提出OpenRef基准，包含三种复杂场景（多样视觉域、变目标数、丰富词汇类型）；引入F1和新指标N3R评估负样本拒绝可靠性；提出训练无关的即插即用策略MCC，通过一致性自验证增强模型性能。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法显著提升了现有REC模型在复杂场景下的表现，为开放世界REC奠定基础。

Conclusion: OpenRef基准有效弥补了现有REC评估的不足，MCC策略以零额外训练成本提升了模型鲁棒性，推动REC向开放世界发展。

Abstract: Referring expression comprehension (REC) aims to localize a target object within an image based on a given expression. Although recent advances in vision-language models have led to substantial improvements in REC tasks, current REC benchmarks often hold simple scenarios and the assumption that each expression maps to a unique object. These limitations hinder the deployment of REC models in open-world environments. To fill this gap, we introduce OpenRef, a new benchmark for REC in complex visual and linguistic scenarios. OpenRef features three key advancements: 1) Diverse visual scenarios: spanning diverse visual domains, including ground views, drone views, dark scenes and adverse weather conditions; 2) Variable target counts: breaking the single-target limitation with multi-target and none-target samples; 3) Rich vocabulary types: incorporating proper nouns, polysemous words and ordinal terms to fit a wider range of expression needs. Furthermore, as traditional metrics are insufficient for open-world setting, we leverage F1 to measure grounding accuracy and propose N3R (Negative Relative Rejection Reliability) to assess relative rejection reliability against negative expressions. Finally, we introduce Multi-task Consistency Checker (MCC), a training-free but plug-and-play strategy that enhances model performance with one click by enforcing consistency self-verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this work significantly advances the performance of existing REC models in complex scenarios, paving the way for open-world REC. Project page: https://zongjianwu.github.io/openref

</details>


### [154] [CMAP: Cross-Modal Adaptive Prompting for Multi-Domain Task-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25708)
*Sriram Mandalika*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于冻结视觉-语言模型（CLIP）的多领域任务增量学习方法，通过文本空间任务路由、多原型视觉-文本置信度与对称跨模态门控三个创新点，在无需任务身份和额外数据的条件下，在MTIL基准上以仅2.5M可训练参数超越现有最优方法5.0、3.7和3.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效的多领域任务增量学习方法均依赖视觉特征进行任务路由、置信度估计和编码器适配，完全忽略了CLIP跨模态文本嵌入空间的潜力。

Method: 1) 文本空间任务路由：用余弦相似度替换视觉高斯匹配，与冻结的CLIP文本原型进行比对，实现零参数且顺序无关的任务路由，对数据稀疏场景鲁棒。2) 多原型视觉-文本置信度：用K-means视觉原型和跨模态对齐分数替代单高斯类建模，并引入任务校准阈值。3) 对称跨模态门控：将逐层Gumbel门控扩展到文本编码器，以批图像特征为条件，保持跨模态对齐。

Result: 在MTIL基准（11个数据集、1201个类别）上，Order-I设置下达到74.2% Transfer、80.5% Average、88.7% Last，分别超越SOTA 5.0、3.7、3.0个百分点，仅2.5M可训练参数，无需外部数据。

Conclusion: 利用CLIP的跨模态文本嵌入空间能显著提升多领域任务增量学习的性能，同时保持参数高效和无需任务身份的先验知识。

Abstract: Multi-domain task-incremental learning requires a model to sequentially acquire knowledge across visually diverse domains without forgetting prior tasks, and without access to task identity at inference. Parameter-efficient methods built on frozen vision-language models have made strong progress, yet all existing approaches rely exclusively on visual features for task routing, confidence estimation, and encoder adaptation, leaving CLIP's cross-modal text embedding space entirely unexploited. We address this gap through three contributions. Text-space task routing replaces visual Gaussian matching with cosine similarity to frozen CLIP text prototypes, giving order-independent routing robust to data scarcity at zero parameter cost. Multi-prototype visual-textual confidence replaces single-Gaussian class modeling with K-means visual prototypes and cross-modal alignment scores under task-calibrated thresholds. Symmetric cross-modal gating extends per-layer Gumbel gates to the text encoder conditioned on batch image features, preserving cross-modal alignment on out-of-distribution inputs. On the MTIL benchmark spanning 11 datasets and 1201 classes, our method achieves 74.2% Transfer, 80.5% Average, and 88.7% Last under Order-I, surpassing the prior state of the art by 5.0, 3.7, and 3.0 percentage points with only 2.5M trainable parameters and no external data.

</details>


### [155] [TriDP-PTM: a three-stage distortion-perception tradeoff guides the pre-training model for radar cardiac sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25725)
*Jinye Li,Aidong Men,Yang Liu,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriDP-PTM，一种雷达-ECG双路径预训练框架，通过三阶段失真-感知权衡分析，在非接触式心脏监测中实现了临床可解释的ECG形态与高下游任务精度（波形分割mIoU 0.80，分类准确率98.3%，血压回归MAE降低56%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非接触式雷达心脏监测方法存在"失真驱动"与"感知驱动"的权衡困境：低失真方法缺乏语义信息，高感知保真度方法可解释性差。需要一种能同时兼顾临床可解释性与下游任务精度的框架。

Method: 提出三阶段失真-感知预训练模型（TriDP-PTM），采用雷达多尺度融合双路径架构：一条为直接"雷达到任务"路径，另一条为间接"雷达到ECG再到任务"路径。通过ECG生成器与特征判别器构成复合损失函数，将ECG形态与节律等医学先验融入下游任务，并基于实证分析划分三个权衡阶段（正和、竞争、负和）。

Result: 在30名受试者5种生理状态的数据集上，间接路径在波形分割（mIoU 0.80）、四任务平均分类准确率（98.3%）和血压回归（MAE降低56%）上均显著优于直接路径及最强基线。

Conclusion: 间接雷达-ECG路径中，在竞争阶段（coopetitive regime）适当权衡失真与感知损失，对同时获得临床可解释的ECG形态与高下游任务精度至关重要。该框架验证了引入医学先验（ECG）进行中间表示学习的有效性。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases (CVDs) remain a leading cause of death globally, necessitating continuous, accurate non-invasive cardiac monitoring. While non-contact radar-based approaches show great promise, they often employ a single "distortion-driven" or "perception-driven" paradigm, frequently facing a trade-off between "low distortion but weak semantic information" and "high perceptual fidelity but poor interpretability." To address this, we propose a Three-stage Distortion-Perception Pre-Training Model (TriDP-PTM), a radar-based multi-scale fusion dual-path framework that systematically compares the "direct radar-to-task" path against an "indirect radar-to-ECG-to-task" path. By integrating an ECG generator with a feature discriminator to form a composite loss function, our approach effectively incorporates medical priors - such as ECG morphology and rhythm - into downstream tasks. Through empirical analysis, we reveal that this trade-off manifests in three distinct phases (Positive-Sum, Coopetitive, and Negative-Sum), showing optimal downstream clinical accuracy typically emerges in the coopetitive stage. Extensive experiments on a dataset involving 30 subjects across 5 physiological states reveal that the indirect path consistently outperforms the direct path in diverse tasks, achieving 0.80 mean IoU in waveform segmentation, 98.3% average classification accuracy across four tasks, and a 56% MAE reduction in blood pressure regression compared to the strongest baselines. These findings validate our framework and indicate that, within the indirect radar-to-ECG pathway, appropriately weighting distortion and perception losses to operate in the coopetitive regime is critical for achieving both clinically interpretable ECG morphology and strong downstream accuracy in non-contact cardiac monitoring.

</details>


### [156] [DeCoDrift: Stabilizing Decoder Coupling in Closed-Loop Foundation Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25730)
*H. M. Shadman Tabib,Md. Shamsuzzoha Bayzid,M Sohel Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示并解决了SAM等基础分割模型在迭代反馈闭环中出现的"解码器耦合漂移"故障模式——跨注意力逐渐偏离目标物体导致误差累积，并提出无需训练和真值的推理阶段稳定框架DeCoDrift，通过近端锚定约束提示更新来保持解码器耦合，显著提升注意力稳定性、时间一致性和分割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础分割模型（如SAM）在迭代流水线中被广泛使用（预测掩码作为下一轮提示），这种闭环动态系统的解码器级行为尚未被充分研究。作者发现反馈回路会引发一个被忽视的故障模式——解码器耦合漂移，即掩码解码器的跨注意力逐渐偏离目标物体，导致误差逐轮累积。

Method: 1) 通过仪器化SAM的掩码解码器，导出无需真值的度量指标（提示-图像耦合、注意力稳定性、时间一致性）来研究漂移现象；2) 将迭代提示形式化为离散时间动力系统，证明近端锚定可减少反馈环中的误差放大；3) 提出DeCoDrift——无需训练和真值的推理阶段稳定框架，通过约束提示更新来保持解码器跨轮次的耦合。

Result: 在体积电子显微镜数据上，标准迭代提示会系统性地退化注意力对齐和时间一致性；DeCoDrift在广泛实验中持续改善注意力稳定性、时间一致性和分割质量，且无需重新训练或真值监督。

Conclusion: 解码器内部动力学不仅是诊断性的，更提供了可操作的信号来稳定闭环使用中的基础分割模型。

Abstract: Foundation segmentation models such as Segment Anything Model (SAM) are now routinely used in iterative pipelines, where each predicted mask is fed back as the next prompt. This practice turns segmentation into a closed-loop dynamical process, yet the decoder-level behavior of these systems remains largely unexamined. We show that this feedback loop can induce a previously overlooked failure mode, decoder coupling drift, in which the mask decoder's cross-attention progressively loses alignment with the target object, causing errors to accumulate across iterations. We study this phenomenon by instrumenting SAM's mask decoder and deriving ground-truth-free measures of prompt-image coupling, attention stability, and temporal consistency. On volumetric electron microscopy data, these decoder-internal signals reveal that standard iterative prompting systematically degrades attention alignment and temporal coherence relative to oracle-anchored feedback. We then formalize iterative prompting as a discrete-time dynamical system and show how proximal anchoring reduces error amplification in the feedback loop. Building on this analysis, we introduce DeCoDrift, a training-free inference-time stabilization framework that constrains prompt updates and preserves decoder coupling across iterations. Across extensive experiments, DeCoDrift consistently improves attention stability, temporal coherence, and segmentation quality over standard iterative prompting, without retraining or ground-truth supervision. More broadly, our results show that decoder-internal dynamics are not merely diagnostic: they provide actionable signals for stabilizing foundation segmentation models in closed-loop use.

</details>


### [157] [Broadband Hyperspectral 3D Imaging using Dispersed Structured Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25757)
*Suhyun Shin,Yunseong Moon,Ryota Maeda,David Lindell,Kyros Kutulacos,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种宽带高光谱3D成像（BH3D）方法，使用单光谱仪系统（可见光+SWIR立体相机），在450-1500 nm范围内同时重建高光谱反射率和精确3D几何结构，并通过多种真实场景验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱3D成像局限于可见光窄波段，无法捕获SWIR波段提供的亚表面特性和材料组成等互补物理信息，而实现宽带高光谱3D成像面临可见光硅传感器与SWIR InGaAs传感器之间的根本性约束，需要复杂的多光谱仪设计。

Method: 提出单光谱仪BH3D系统，采用可见光与SWIR相机组成的立体装置，将分散结构光扩展到宽带模式；对宽带分散结构光的图像形成进行建模，并联合估计高光谱反射率和深度。

Result: 在多种真实场景中实现了高精度重建，平均光谱角度映射器0.13 rad，均方根误差0.03，平均深度误差4.5 mm。成功展示了同色异谱材料识别、不透明层成像、钞票隐藏特征揭示以及血管可视化等应用。

Conclusion: 该方法有效突破了传统高光谱3D成像的波段限制，仅用单光谱仪即可实现宽带范围的高光谱反射率与3D几何联合重建，显著拓展了实际应用场景。

Abstract: Hyperspectral 3D imaging enables the capture of dense spectral information and scene geometry but has traditionally been confined to narrow spectral windows, typically the visible range. In this work, we introduce a broadband hyperspectral 3D imaging (BH3D) method to extend this capability across the full visible-near-infrared and short-wavelength infrared (SWIR) spectrum (450-1500 nm). This broad coverage is critical as it captures complementary physical cues: visible wavelengths reveal surface appearance, while SWIR bands provide insight into subsurface properties and material composition. However, realizing BH3D is challenging due to fundamental sensor constraints between visible-spectrum silicon and SWIR-spectrum InGaAs sensors, which necessitate complex multi-spectrograph designs. Here we propose a single-spectrograph BH3D system, using a stereo setup comprising visible and SWIR cameras, that reconstructs dense broadband hyperspectral reflectance together with accurate 3D geometry. Our key idea is to extend dispersed structured light to the broadband regime using a single spectrograph. We model the image formation of broadband dispersed structured light, and estimate hyperspectral reflectance and depth. We validate our approach on diverse real-world scenes, demonstrating accurate reconstruction with a mean spectral angle mapper of 0.13 rad, root mean square error of 0.03, and mean depth error of 4.5 mm. We further demonstrate identifying metameric materials, performing imaging through opaque layers, uncovering hidden features on banknotes, and revealing blood vessels.

</details>


### [158] [Towards Anatomically Plausible Human Image Generation via Synthetic Localized Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25759)
*Bao Li,Yuliang Xiu,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ASAP框架，通过合成解剖偏好数据和局部边际DPO算法，有效减少文本到图像基础模型生成人体图像时的解剖结构错误，同时保持整体图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型在生成人体图像时常出现解剖结构错误（如肢体缺失、关节错位等）。监督微调方法依赖有限的高质量人体照片数据集，且因光照、姿态、背景等混杂因素导致优化信号模糊。偏好对齐方法（如DPO）将所有像素等同对待，无法利用解剖伪影的局部性特征。

Method: (1) 提出局部退化机制：在高保真人体图像的目标区域引入显式解剖错误，构建受控偏好对；(2) 构建HAP数据集（超过1万对）；(3) 提出局部边际DPO变体：优先优化目标解剖区域，同时设置有限偏好边际防止过度优化、保持全局语义；(4) 提出HAF-Bench基准用于系统评估解剖保真度。

Result: 在多个基础模型上的大量实验表明，ASAP能够一致地减少解剖错误，同时保持整体图像质量。

Conclusion: 局部合成的受控偏好数据结合针对性的偏好优化算法，是提升生成人体图像解剖正确性的有效途径，且不牺牲全局图像质量。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image foundation models have achieved remarkable visual realism, yet generating human images with correct anatomical structures remains challenging. Existing approaches enforce anatomical constraints through part-specific modules or localized loss weighting during supervised fine-tuning on high-quality human photos, but such datasets are limited and often provide ambiguous optimization signals due to confounding factors such as lighting, pose, and background. Preference-based alignment offers an alternative, but standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) treats all pixels equally and therefore fails to exploit the localized nature of anatomical artifacts. To address this, we propose the framework of Alignment via Synthetic Anatomical Preference (ASAP), which constructs controlled preference pairs through a localized degradation mechanism applied to high-fidelity human images. This mechanism performs a controlled experiment on images by introducing explicit anatomical errors in targeted regions while preserving the remaining content. With this mechanism, we create the Human Anatomical Preference (HAP) dataset with over 10K curated pairs for effective anatomical alignment of text-to-image human image generative models. To better leverage the locality of these controlled preference pairs, we introduce a localized and margin-bounded variant of DPO that prioritizes optimization in targeted anatomical regions while enforcing a finite preference margin to prevent over-optimization and preserve global semantics. We further introduce HAF-Bench, a benchmark for systematic evaluation of anatomical fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASAP consistently reduces anatomical errors across multiple foundation models while maintaining overall image quality.

</details>


### [159] [AI-T2I: Aggregating-and-Isolating Cross-Attention to Diffusion Models for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25763)
*Shipeng Cao,Biao Qian,Haipeng Liu,Yang Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-T2I方法，通过聚合损失（Aggregation Loss）解决同一主体内令牌激活分散问题，并通过隔离损失（Isolation Loss）拉开不同主体令牌激活距离，从而提升扩散模型在文本到图像生成中的精确对齐能力。实验表明该方法在多个基准上超越现有SOTA，并具有良好的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型在交叉注意力机制中难以实现精确的文本-图像对齐。已有工作主要关注不同主体令牌之间激活重叠问题，却忽略了同一主体令牌内部激活分散的问题。

Method: 提出AI-T2I方法：(1) 聚合损失（Aggregation Loss）：识别并整合同一令牌内分散的激活值，间接缓解重叠问题；(2) 隔离损失（Isolation Loss）：进一步将不同令牌的激活值彼此拉开，实现精确对齐。

Result: 在多个基准上超越现有SOTA方法，并在可控布局生成、个性化生成等下游任务中展现出优秀的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的聚合-隔离交叉注意力机制能够有效解决令牌内激活分散和令牌间激活重叠问题，实现更精确的文本到图像对齐，且方法具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Text-to-image synthesis has made significant progress, benefiting from the strong generative capabilities of diffusion models. However, these models struggle to achieve precise text-to-image alignment within cross-attention maps during the denoising process. Existing works primarily focus on inter-subject-token activations (i.e., cross-attention scores) overlap for different subjects, overlooking the intra-subject-token activations scattering issue for identical subjects. In this paper, we propose an Aggregating-and-Isolating cross-attention approach to diffusion models for Text-to-Image synthesis, dubbed AI-T2I. Technically, to address the scattering issue, we devise an aggregation loss to identify and consolidate the scattered intra-token activations, which implicitly helps mitigate the potential overlap issue. Upon that, an isolation loss is further introduced to push the inter-token activations apart, thus fulfilling precise text-to-image alignment. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of AI-T2I over the state-of-the-art works for text-to-image synthesis. Furthermore, our AI-T2I exhibits excellent generalization across other tasks, e.g., controllable layout generation and personalized generation.

</details>


### [160] [Benchmarking Pathology Foundation Models for Spatial Domain Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25764)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Yuanchi Zhu,Hanqing Chao,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaPath-Bench是一个用于诊断病理基础模型空间表征能力的基准，通过在配对的全切片图像和空间转录组数据上评估19种编码器和7种空间域识别方法，揭示了不同预训练范式捕获组织空间架构的不同方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的病理基础模型评估主要基于下游临床任务指标，这些任务级评估无法揭示模型表征本身编码了什么信息，特别是无法判断PFM嵌入是否能够区分有意义的组织区域并捕捉它们的空间关系。

Method: SpaPath-Bench将空间域识别(SDI)作为诊断任务，在42张公开配对的全切片图像(WSI)和空间转录组(ST)数据上，对19种编码器和7种SDI方法进行大规模评估，并使用无监督空间一致性、转录组参考一致性和专家参考一致性三种互补标准来衡量分区质量。

Result: 通过83K次运行实验，SpaPath-Bench发现不同的预训练范式能够捕捉组织空间架构的不同方面，为构建下一代空间感知计算病理模型提供了实用指导。

Conclusion: SpaPath-Bench作为表征级别的基准，能够有效诊断病理基础模型的空间表征能力，弥补了现有任务级评估的不足，并揭示了不同预训练范式在空间表征上的差异。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) have emerged as a core approach for learning transferable representations from whole slide images (WSIs), and they are typically benchmarked through downstream clinical endpoints. While such task level evaluations are indispensable, they offer limited insight into what the representations themselves encode, particularly whether PFM embeddings can distinguish meaningful tissue regions and capture their spatial relationships. We present SpaPath-Bench, a representation level benchmark designed to diagnose spatial representation capability in PFMs. SpaPath-Bench formulates spatial domain identification (SDI) on paired whole slide image and spatial transcriptomics (ST) data as a diagnostic task. It curates 42 public paired WSI and ST slides, enables large scale evaluation across 19 encoders and seven SDI methods, and measures partition quality using three complementary criteria: unsupervised spatial coherence, transcriptomics referenced agreement, and expert referenced agreement. Across 83K runs, SpaPath-Bench reveals that different pretraining paradigms capture distinct aspects of tissue spatial architecture, and it provides practical guidance for building the next generation of spatially aware computational pathology models. Code and data pipelines are publicly available at https://bokai-zhao.github.io/SpaPath-benchboard/.

</details>


### [161] [Concept Unlearning via Cross-Attention Activation Projection for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25765)
*Saemi Moon,Suhyeon Jun,Seoyeon Lee,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PURE是一种闭式概念擦除方法，通过在U-Net交叉注意力激活空间中构建遗忘和保留基，并应用线性投影器编辑注意力权重，在保持保留概念的同时，显著减少目标概念在释义和对抗性提示下的泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭式概念擦除方法仅通过文本编码器对少数锚定提示的响应表示目标概念，导致模型在面对释义提示时无法有效擦除。作者认为目标概念应在交叉注意力激活空间中表示，因为该空间描述的是模型即将渲染的内容，能更好地泛化到未覆盖的释义模板。

Method: PURE方法：从短去噪轨迹中捕获每层交叉注意力激活构建遗忘和保留基，然后对交叉注意力键和值权重应用单个线性投影器，实现闭式编辑。

Result: 在涵盖艺术风格、知识产权、名人、NSFW等十个概念的概念擦除基准上，PURE在释义和对抗性提示下显著降低了目标泄漏，同时保留概念接近未编辑模型，取得了最佳的遗忘-保留权衡。

Conclusion: 在交叉注意力激活空间中表示目标概念比在文本嵌入空间中更有效，PURE通过这种方式实现了更好的泛化能力和遗忘-保留平衡。

Abstract: Concept unlearning aims to erase a target concept from a pretrained text-to-image diffusion model without retraining. Closed-form methods are attractive in this setting because they apply a single deterministic edit to the cross-attention weights and add no inference-time cost. Existing closed-form methods, however, represent the target concept through the text encoder's response to a few short anchor prompts that name it, and paraphrased prompts that evoke the concept without naming it consistently bypass the edit. We argue that the target should instead be represented in the cross-attention activation space. Text embeddings describe the user's prompt, while cross-attention activations describe what the model is about to render, and the latter generalize to paraphrase the anchor templates do not cover. Building on this observation, we propose PURE (Projection in U-Net Rendering for Erasure), a closed-form method that builds the forget and retain bases from per-layer cross-attention activations captured along a short denoising trajectory and applies a single linear projector to the cross-attention key and value weights. On a recent holistic concept-unlearning benchmark covering ten concepts across artistic style, intellectual property, celebrity, and NSFW categories, PURE significantly reduces target leakage under paraphrased and adversarial prompts while preserving retain concepts close to the unedited model, yielding the best overall forget-retain trade-off among evaluated methods.

</details>


### [162] [SAFE-Diff: Scale-Aware Attention and Feature-Dispersive Diffusion with Uncertainty Estimation for Contrast-Enhanced Breast MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25767)
*Tianyu Zhang,Xinglong Liang,Jarek van Dijk,Luyi Han,Chunyao Lu,Antonio Portaluri,Xinghe Xie,Yaofei Duan,Nika Rasoolzadeh,Xin Wang,Yuan Gao,Muzhen He,Yue Sun,Jonas Teuwen,Tao Tan,Ritse Mann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于合成高保真度对比增强MRI的临床价值与挑战，指出其在实现更安全高效的乳腺癌筛查方面具有重要意义，但面临复杂的病变纹理和异质性增强模式等技术难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成高保真度对比增强MRI在乳腺癌筛查中具有重要的临床价值，能够实现更安全、更高效的筛查流程，避免患者接触造影剂带来的风险。

Method: 该研究致力于开发能够处理复杂病变纹理和异质性增强模式的合成方法，具体技术细节需进一步查看全文。

Result: 尚需查看完整论文以获取具体实验结果和定量评估指标。

Conclusion: 合成高保真对比增强MRI在乳腺癌筛查中具有重要的临床转化潜力，但解决复杂病变纹理和异质性增强模式是关键技术挑战。

Abstract: Synthesizing high fidelity contrast enhanced MRI is clinically valuable for safer and more efficient breast cancer screening, yet remains challenging due to complex lesion textures and heterogeneous enhancement patterns.

</details>


### [163] [DRFusion: Drift-Resilient Temporally Consistent Infrared-Visible Video Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25775)
*Xingyuan Li,Haoyuan Xu,Shulin Li,Xiang Chen,Zhiying Jiang,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种抗漂移的红外与可见光视频融合方法，通过历史条件运动生成、稳定历史引导、软时间锚定和解耦结构-运动适配策略，实现时空一致的融合效果，在融合质量和时间稳定性上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外-可见光视频融合方法存在两大问题：(1) 基于光流的方法几何刚性大、易产生鬼影；(2) 扩散模型逐帧处理扩展至自回归设置时缺乏时序约束，导致误差累积和漂移。因此需要一种具有内在时序约束、抗漂移的融合方法。

Method: (1) 将视频融合重定义为历史条件运动生成任务；(2) 稳定历史引导与软时间锚定：将时序一致性转化为频谱滤波，隐式聚合运动动态，避免刚性对齐；(3) 解耦结构-运动适配策略：通过两阶段训练和潜在空间精化，桥接预训练先验与结构约束。

Result: 在融合质量和时间稳定性两方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 该抗漂移视频融合方法通过将时序一致性建模为频谱滤波、引入解耦的结构-运动适配，有效解决了误差累积和漂移问题，为红外-可见光动态场景融合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Infrared and visible video fusion is essential for achieving comprehensive perception in dynamic scenes. However, maintaining temporal consistency remains a formidable challenge. Conventional methods relying on optical flow often suffer from geometric rigidity and ghosting artifacts. Moreover, standard diffusion-based fusion models typically operate in a frame-by-frame manner; when extended to autoregressive settings, they lack intrinsic temporal constraints and are prone to severe error accumulation and drifting, where minor artifacts amplify over time. To address these limitations, we propose a drift-resilient video fusion method that reformulates the task as history-conditioned motion generation. We introduce Stabilized History Guidance and Soft Temporal Anchoring to reframe temporal consistency as spectral filtering, implicitly aggregating motion dynamics without rigid alignment. Furthermore, our Decoupled Structure-Motion Adaptation strategy bridges pre-trained priors and structural constraints via two-stage training and latent refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both fusion quality and temporal stability.

</details>


### [164] [OMGTex: One-stage Multi-style Facial Texture Reconstruction without Geometry Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25778)
*Zitong Xiao,Yuda Qiu,Zisheng Ye,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OMGTex，一种基于扩散模型的端到端框架，无需3D几何先验即可从多风格人脸图像重建高质量、可编辑的面部UV纹理，通过梯度引导精炼策略和语义感知训练范式解决结构一致性与语义解耦问题，并构建了首个多风格配对纹理数据集CANVAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纹理重建方法存在两个主要局限：(1) 依赖3D几何先验导致脆弱性，尤其在面部遮挡或风格化域中难以准确估计；(2) 缺乏语义解耦能力，无法进行区域特定的纹理编辑和风格迁移。本文旨在同时解决这两个挑战。

Method: (1) 提出无几何管线，直接将2D人脸图像映射到可编辑UV纹理；(2) 引入梯度引导推理时精炼策略，纠正扩散生成中常见的UV未对齐问题；(3) 利用扩散模型固有语义分布能力，设计新型训练范式增强语义感知纹理编辑；(4) 构建CANVAS数据集，首个涵盖真实和多样化风格化域的配对纹理重建数据集。

Result: 在多个面部纹理基准上达到了最先进的性能，实现了鲁棒、风格一致且可编辑的面部纹理重建。

Conclusion: OMGTex是首个无几何推理框架，能够在多样化域中实现鲁棒、风格一致且可编辑的面部纹理重建，为多风格面部纹理重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose OMGTex, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework for reconstructing high-quality and editable facial UV textures from multi-style facial images. Existing texture reconstruction methods face two major limitations: (1) Fragility due to reliance on 3D geometry priors, which are difficult to estimate accurately, especially under facial occlusions or in stylized domains; and (2) A lack of semantic disentanglement, inhibiting region-specific texture editing and style transfer. Our work addresses both challenges simultaneously.
  Our core innovation is a geometry-free pipeline that directly maps a 2D face image to its corresponding editable UV texture. We introduce two key techniques: First, to address the challenge of UV misalignment common in diffusion generation, we introduce a gradient-guided refinement strategy at inference time, which explicitly corrects structural consistency. Second, we leverage the inherent semantic distribution capability of diffusion models and design a novel training paradigm to enhance this tendency, enabling semantic-aware editing of facial texture. Furthermore, to address the data scarcity in multi-style texture reconstruction, we construct CANVAS, the first comprehensive paired texture reconstruction dataset covering realistic and diverse stylized domains.
  To the best of our knowledge, OMGTex is the first geometry-free inference framework that achieves robust, style-consistent, and editable facial texture reconstruction across diverse domains. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple facial texture benchmarks.

</details>


### [165] [Addressing Exacerbated Attention Sink for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25799)
*Shuai Yi,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了跨域小样本学习（CDFSL）中注意力汇（attention sink）问题被标准微调加剧的现象，将其解释为模型域适应的捷径学习，并提出动态重加权token的方法来抑制简单token、增强难token学习，在四个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（如CLIP）在跨域小样本学习中的潜力尚未被充分挖掘，且注意力汇现象在CDFSL场景中的作用未被研究；标准微调会加剧注意力汇问题，导致类间判别性差。

Method: 提出Token Importance Re-weighting (TIR)方法，在目标域微调过程中根据token与目标域类别的相关性动态重新加权，抑制简单token的依赖并增强难token的学习，减少注意力汇token并提升判别性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上验证了方法的合理性，取得了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 注意力汇现象是CDFSL中模型采取捷径学习进行域适应的结果；通过动态token重加权可以有效缓解该问题，提升跨域小样本学习性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) like CLIP have shown impressive generalization capabilities, yet their potential for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning (CDFSL) remains underexplored, where the model needs to transfer source-domain information to target domains with scarce training data. While the attention sink phenomenon has been observed in VLMs for certain tasks, its role in CDFSL scenarios has not been studied. In this paper, we uncover a critical issue overlooked by prior works: standard target-domain few-shot fine-tuning in CDFSL significantly exacerbates the attention sink problem, leading to poor discriminability across classes. To understand this phenomenon, through extensive experiments, we interpret it as the model's shortcut learning for domain adaptation: to overcome the huge domain gap between the source and target domains, the model shows a high tendency to push tokens that are initially closer to target-domain classes (i.e., simple tokens) to be even closer to these classes, exacerbating the attention sink and wasting the capability of learning other discriminative but initially further tokens (i.e., hard tokens). To address this, we propose a novel approach to dynamically re-weight tokens according to their relevance with target-domain classes during the target-domain finetuning, which explicitly suppresses the model's reliance on these simple tokens and enhances the learning of hard tokens, reducing sink tokens and enhancing discriminability. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets validate the rationale of our method, demonstrating new state-of-the-art performance. Our codes are available at https://github.com/shuaiyi308/TIR.

</details>


### [166] [PixelWizard: Towards Efficient High-Fidelity Video Generation at Ultra-Large Spatial Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25801)
*Wenxue Li,Jingjing Ren,Peng Zhang,Tian Ye,Daiguo Zhou,Jian Luan,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelWizard提出了一种层次化解耦框架，通过紧凑时空锚点分离全局结构建模与细粒度细节合成，并引入噪声跨度对齐捷径训练，实现2K/4K视频生成加速超10倍，同时保持结构稳定性和高视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频生成面临优化不稳定和计算成本高昂的双重瓶颈。token序列的巨量扩展导致优化偏向局部纹理而牺牲全局一致性，引发结构崩溃，同时带来高昂的训练成本和严重的推理延迟。

Method: 1) 层次化解耦框架：先建立紧凑时空锚点集中稠密结构先验，再引导高分辨率下的细粒度生成；2) Noise-Span Aligned Shortcut Training：通过显式建模步长，使模型能以大步长遍历生成轨迹；3) Exponential Index-Biased Sampling和Adaptive Noise-Span Calibration：对齐高分辨率网格的偏移噪声调度，实现无需蒸馏的鲁棒少步推理。

Result: PixelWizard在实现优越视觉质量的同时，将原生2K/4K视频的生成采样速度提升了超过10倍。

Conclusion: 通过层次化解耦全局结构与细节合成，并结合噪声跨度对齐的捷径训练，有效解决了高分辨率视频生成中的优化不稳定和计算瓶颈问题，实现了高质量与高效率的统一。

Abstract: High-resolution video generation faces a coupled bottleneck of optimization instability and prohibitive computational costs. The massive expansion of the token sequence not only biases optimization toward local textures at the expense of global coherence, leading to structural collapse, but also imposes prohibitive training costs and severe inference latency. To address this, we propose PixelWizard, a framework that hierarchically decouples global structure modeling from fine-grained detail synthesis. PixelWizard first establishes a compact spatiotemporal anchor to concentrate dense structural priors, which then guides fine-grained generation at high resolution. This mitigates the local optimization bias to ensure structural stability without compromising high-frequency details. Leveraging this structural stability, we introduce Noise-Span Aligned Shortcut Training to break the inference bottleneck. By explicitly modeling the step size, this mechanism allows the model to traverse the generation trajectory with large steps. Crucially, we incorporate Exponential Index-Biased Sampling and Adaptive Noise-Span Calibration to align optimization with the shifted noise schedules of high-resolution grids, ensuring robust few-step inference without incurring the heavy overhead of distillation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PixelWizard achieves superior visual quality while accelerating the generative sampling of native 2K/4K videos by over 10x.

</details>


### [167] [Rethinking VLM Representation for VLA Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25802)
*Weifeng Lin,Siyuan Huang,Hao Li,Tingwei Chen,Ruichuan An,Xinyu Wei,Jianbo Liu,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了VLA初始化的表示设计问题，发现预训练VLM表示是动作性能的关键来源；具身VQA适应的收益取决于下游瓶颈且不具可加性；LoRA比全微调更可靠，能保留预训练表示；机器人数据预训练进一步提升性能，最优方案是基于LoRA的分阶段训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型广泛使用预训练VLM作为策略骨干，但何种VLM表示对VLA初始化有用尚不明确，亟需系统研究。

Method: 沿三个维度（具身VQA监督的能力级别、参数更新策略、机器人数据预训练）进行受控表示设计实验，系统评估各因素对VLA初始化的影响。

Result: 1) 预训练VLM表示是动作性能的关键来源；2) 具身VQA适应收益不统一，取决于下游瓶颈且不同能力域收益不可加；3) LoRA比全微调更可靠，过度重塑预训练表示会削弱初始化；4) 机器人数据预训练进一步提升性能，分阶段LoRA训练最优。

Conclusion: 有效的VLM到VLA适配应在注入动作相关的具身和机器人轨迹信号的同时，保留对动作学习仍有用的预训练VLM表示。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models widely adopt pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as policy backbones, yet it remains unclear what kind of pretrained VLM representation is useful as a VLA initialization. In this paper, we study VLA initialization as a controlled representation-design problem along three axes: capability-level embodied VQA supervision, parameter-update strategy, and robot-data pretraining. Our experiments show that the original pretrained VLM representation is a key source of action performance. However, embodied VQA adaptation does not yield uniform gains: its benefit depends on downstream bottlenecks, and gains from different capability domains are not simply additive. For update strategy, LoRA provides a more reliable initialization than Full Finetune, indicating that overly reshaping the pretrained representation can weaken VLA initialization. Robot-data pretraining further improves VLA initialization, with the strongest variant obtained by staged LoRA-based training. Together, these findings suggest that effective VLM-to-VLA adaptation should inject action-relevant embodied and robot-trajectory signals while preserving the pretrained VLM representation that remains useful for action learning.

</details>


### [168] [ATV-Net: Adaptive Triple-View Network with Dynamic Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25803)
*Hsin-Jui Pan,Sheng-Wei Chan,Meng-Qian Li,Chun-Po Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ATV-Net，通过仅修改分割头，使用三种互补的感受野视图（微观、局部、侦察）和自适应决策门动态融合，在Cityscapes上达到80.31% mIoU，证明经典CNN分割管线仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义分割研究趋向于更强的上下文建模、密集注意力和Transformer架构，但经典CNN分割管线因其简洁性、高效性和易实现性仍具吸引力。作者希望探究仅通过修改分割头，能将ResNet分割模型提升到何种程度。

Method: 提出ATV-Net，包含：(1) 三种互补感受野视图——微观视图捕获逐点语义响应、局部视图建模邻域结构和目标边界、侦察视图提供扩大的上下文线索；(2) 自适应决策门动态选择感受野响应；(3) 紧凑的全局协调层提升空间和语义一致性。

Result: 在Cityscapes验证集上达到80.31% mIoU。

Conclusion: 经典CNN分割管线远未过时，通过简单的感受野视图和自适应融合，基于ResNet的管线无需Transformer风格的全局注意力或过于复杂的上下文模块，即可达到有竞争力的精度。

Abstract: Recent semantic segmentation research has increasingly moved toward stronger context modeling, dense attention, and transformer-based architectures. Although these models achieve impressive performance, classical CNN-based segmentation pipelines remain attractive because of their simplicity, efficiency, and ease of implementation. This paper revisits a practical question: how far can a ResNet-based segmentation model be improved by only modifying the segmentation head? We propose ATV-Net, an Adaptive Triple-View Network that strengthens a ResNet-101 backbone using three simple but complementary receptive-field views. The micro view captures point-wise semantic responses, the local view models neighborhood structures and object boundaries, and the scout view provides enlarged contextual cues. Instead of fusing these views with fixed weights, ATV-Net introduces an Adaptive Decision Gate that dynamically selects receptive-field responses according to input scene characteristics. A compact global coordination layer is further applied to improve spatial and semantic consistency. Experiments on the Cityscapes validation set show that ATV-Net achieves 80.31\% mIoU. This result suggests that classical CNN-based segmentation is still far from obsolete: with simple receptive-field views and adaptive fusion, a ResNet-based pipeline can reach a competitive accuracy level without relying on transformer-style global attention or overly complex context modules.

</details>


### [169] [Event-to-Video Reconstruction using Spatio-Temporal and Frequency-Enhanced Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25804)
*Ramna Maqsood,Paulo Nunes,Luís Ducla Soares,Caroline Conti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MSFET-E2V，一种多尺度频率增强Transformer模型，通过跨域注意力模块融合时空特征与离散小波变换频率表征，并设计轻量级小波增强跳跃块，在事件到视频重建任务中实现优于现有方法的性能，同时减少参数量、GPU内存和推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率、低延迟和节能优势，但缺乏密集强度帧限制了传统视觉方法的直接应用。现有基于CNN和Transformer的E2V重建方法主要在空间域操作，难以恢复精细结构细节并抑制严重重建伪影。

Method: 提出MSFET-E2V模型，核心包括：（1）跨域注意力模块，融合时空特征与离散小波变换的频率感知表示；（2）轻量级小波增强跳跃块，通过联合空间-频率域处理实现伪影抑制和结构细节细化。

Result: 在多个真实世界事件数据集上，MSFET-E2V在重建质量上优于现有最先进方法，同时显著减少了参数量、GPU内存使用和推理时间。

Conclusion: 通过引入频域信息与跨域注意力机制，MSFET-E2V有效解决了E2V重建中细节恢复和伪影抑制的难题，在性能和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Event cameras offer significant advantages over conventional frame-based counterparts, including high temporal resolution, low latency, and energy efficiency. These characteristics make them suitable for high-speed and high-dynamic range scene acquisition scenarios; however, the lack of dense intensity frames limits the direct applicability of conventional computer vision methods for scene understanding. Event-to-video (E2V) reconstruction seeks to bridge this gap by converting asynchronous event streams into a sequence of synchronous video frames. Existing E2V reconstruction methods based on convolutional neural networks and transformers operate primarily in the spatial domain and often struggle to recover fine structural details while suppressing severe reconstruction artifacts. To address these issues, we propose MSFET-E2V, a novel multiscale frequency-enhanced transformer model. At its core lies a cross-domain attention module, which fuses spatio-temporal features with frequency-aware representations derived from the discrete wavelet transform. Unlike prior methods relying solely on spatial attention, our approach effectively captures both local and global structures by taking into account low- and high-frequency components, enhancing detail preservation and robustness across various motion scenarios. Furthermore, we propose a lightweight wavelet-enhanced skip block that serves as a skip connection, facilitating artifact suppression and structural detail refinement through joint spatial-frequency domain processing. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MSFET-E2V achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art methods on multiple real-world event datasets, offering significant gains in reconstruction quality. Moreover, compared to the existing transformer-based method, our proposed model significantly reduces the number of parameters, the GPU memory usage, and inference time.

</details>


### [170] [An Analysis Focused on Womens Safety: Can VAD Models Be Enhanced by a Multi-modal Dataset?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25806)
*Sangeeta,Maddikuntla Sai Prajwal,Debi Prosad Dogra,Kamalakar Vijay Thakare,Hyungjoo Jung,Ig-Jae Kim,Heeseung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ExtrAnom数据集，一个专注于女性安全的多模态视频异常检测基准，包含1001个带有文本描述的视频，涵盖5类针对女性的犯罪（跟踪、抢项链、绑架、暗杀、骚扰），包含低光、低分辨率、远镜头等现实场景，每个视频配有4条文本注释，实验表明现有数据集和方法不足以检测针对女性的异常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测数据集（如XD-Violence、UCF-Crime）缺乏针对女性安全场景的特定数据，无法有效捕捉抢项链、跟踪、骚扰等细微犯罪模式，且多基于高分辨率、良好光照条件，不反映真实监控场景中的低光、低分辨率、远镜头等挑战。

Method: 构建ExtrAnom多模态数据集：包含1001个视频（500正常+501异常），分为5类女性犯罪；视频涵盖低光(8%)、低分辨率(13%)、远镜头(15%)和日光(64%)场景；每个视频配4条文本描述（1条人工+3条大模型生成），支持跨模态和视觉语言模型验证。

Result: 在ExtrAnom上对多种单模态和多模态VAD方法进行基准测试，实验表明现有数据集（XD-Violence、UCF-Crime、UCA）训练的模型不足以检测针对女性的异常行为。

Conclusion: ExtrAnom填补了女性安全视频异常检测领域的数据空白，为发展更有效的女性安全监控系统提供了重要基准，但现有方法在该场景下表现不足，仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Women's safety and security are paramount for a modern society. Crimes against women occur in daylight as well as in low-light conditions. Often, such events are captured through real-world surveillance cameras that operate at lower resolutions. Despite substantial progress in CV-related research, video anomaly detection (VAD) focused on women's safety has not yet been adequately addressed. Existing video anomaly datasets contain well-lit, high-resolution, close-shot videos, and fail to represent women-centric anomalies such as chain snatching, stalking, inappropriate touch, and other subtle forms of crime against women. To address these problems, we propose the ExtrAnom dataset, a new multi-modal benchmark containing 1001 videos with textual descriptions, 500 normal and 501 anomalous, classified into 5 different types of women-centric crimes. The dataset comprises low-light (8%), low-resolution videos (13%), long-shot (15%), along with daylight (64%) anomalous videos. And it covers anomalous events like stalking (3.9%), chain snatching (17.6%), kidnapping (7.3%), assassinations (2.3%), harassment (18.9%), and normal (50%). Each video is supplemented with 4 textual annotations, including one human-generated and three LLM-generated descriptions, enabling cross-modal and VLM-based validations. The aim of creating a women-centric dataset is to accurately detect the women-centric anomaly patterns, which are possible to observe visually. The dataset supplements the VLMs to accurately generate video-level descriptions. ExtrAnom has been benchmarked against popular unimodal and multi-modal VAD datasets (e.g., XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, and UCA) and SOTA methods. Experiments reveal that the existing datasets are insufficient to train models for detecting women-centric anomalies.

</details>


### [171] [Data-driven Head Motion Generation through Natural Gaze-Head Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25810)
*Xiaohan Liu,Yilin Wen,Yusuke Sugano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个数据驱动的 gaze-head 时序协调建模方法，利用大规模自然场景面部视频，通过自动管线提取 gaze 和头部运动，构建条件变分自编码器生成与 gaze 相关且多样自然的头部运动，并应用于 gaze 控制的面部视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对 gaze 与头部运动之间时序协调关系的数据驱动建模，且缺乏大规模自然场景下的训练数据。

Method: 1) 自动管线：利用现成的外观 gaze 估计器从自然视频中提取 gaze 和头部运动数据；2) 条件变分自编码器（cVAE）：捕捉 gaze 与头部运动之间的概率相关性及时序动态，生成以 gaze 为条件的多样头部运动。

Result: 人类评估和定量比较表明方法有效，评估者对本文方法的偏好具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了 gaze 控制下的面部视频生成中自然逼真的头部运动，补充了先前被忽视的 gaze-head 协调维度。

Abstract: We present the first data-driven approach to model temporal gaze-head coordination from large-scale in-the-wild facial videos. To obtain training data for generalizable learning, we propose an automatic pipeline that extracts natural yet diverse gaze and head motions with off-the-shelf appearance-based gaze estimators. To capture the probabilistic correlation and temporal dynamics of gaze-head coordination, we build our model on a generative conditional Variational Autoencoder for plausible yet diverse gaze-conditioned head motion generations. We further apply our framework to gaze-controlled facial video generation, where we enable video generation with natural and realistic head motion correlated to the input gaze - an aspect that has not been emphasized before. Human evaluation and quantitative comparisons demonstrate our method's effectiveness and validate our design choices, with evaluators showing statistically significant preference for our approach over baseline methods.

</details>


### [172] [[CLS] is Not Enough: Multi-Label Recognition via Patch-Level Inference and Adaptive Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25821)
*Akang Wang,Xili Deng,Zhanxuan Hu,Yi Zhao,Yonghang Tai,Huafeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIAA是一种针对多标签图像识别的无训练框架，通过补丁级推理和自适应聚合，在不需梯度更新或参数微调的情况下，在NUS-WIDE基准上实现了超过6%的mAP提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型在多标签识别任务中表现不佳，因为单一的[CLS]全局视觉表示无法忠实地编码具有不同尺度、上下文和共现模式的多样目标。

Method: 提出PIAA框架：(i) 通过减轻视觉编码器中的语义纠缠获得更具判别性的补丁表示，并学习无监督视觉分类器以缩小视觉-语言模态差距；(ii) 引入自适应聚合模块将补丁级分数整合为最终多标签预测。整个流程无需梯度更新或参数微调。

Result: 在NUS-WIDE基准上超过代表性基线6%以上的mAP增益，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: PIAA通过补丁级推理与自适应聚合，以无训练方式有效提升了多标签图像识别性能，验证了该方法的高效性和有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models such as CLIP exhibit strong zero-shot recognition capability by aligning images with textual concepts, yet they often underperform on multi-label recognition where multiple objects co-exist. A key bottleneck is that the [CLS] token, as a single global visual representation, is insufficient to faithfully encode diverse targets with varying scales, contexts, and co-occurrence patterns. To address this limitation, we present a new multi-label image recognition framework, termed PIAA, which formulates prediction as Patch-level Inference followed by Adaptive Aggregation. Specifically, we first enhance patch-wise predictions from two complementary perspectives: (i) mitigating semantic entanglement in the visual encoder to obtain more discriminative patch representations, and (ii) learning an unsupervised visual classifier to narrow the vision-language modality gap. We then introduce an adaptive aggregation module that consolidates patch-level scores into the final multi-label prediction. Notably, the entire pipeline is fully training-free, requiring no gradient updates or parameter fine-tuning. Experiments show that our method achieves strong improvements with minimal extra computation, exceeding a 6% mAP gain on the challenging NUS-WIDE benchmark over representative baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/akang-wang/PIAA.

</details>


### [173] [SAM3-Assisted Training of Lightweight YOLO Models for Precision Pig Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25860)
*Marcos Vinicius Mendes Faria,Thiago Borges Pereira,Isabella C. F. S. Condotta,Thiago Meireles Paixão,Francisco de Assis Boldt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全自动知识蒸馏流水线，利用SAM 3生成零样本伪标签来训练高效的YOLOv8目标检测器，在不需人工标注的情况下，在PigLife数据集上实现了79.4%的mAP，并将推理延迟相比教师模型降低约200倍，在低遮挡场景下检测性能接近人工标注基准（AP50 > 99%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度基础模型（如SAM 3）计算量过大难以边缘部署，而轻量模型（如YOLO）需要大量人工标注，两者之间存在难以调和的矛盾，亟需一种无需人工标注即可训练高效边缘部署模型的方法。

Method: 提出全自动知识蒸馏流水线：将SAM 3作为离线自动标注器，生成零样本伪标签，用于训练YOLOv8目标检测器，从而在无需人工干预的情况下完成从教师模型到学生模型的知识迁移。

Result: SAM 3训练的YOLOv8m在PigLife数据集上达到79.4% mAP，推理延迟相比SAM 3降低约200倍；低遮挡场景下AP50 > 99%，接近人工标注基准性能。

Conclusion: 基础模型可以作为有效的零标注成本监督者，使智能农业中的边缘计算解决方案具备可扩展性，为消除深度学习在精准畜牧养殖中部署的人工标注瓶颈提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep learning-based object detection has revolutionized Precision Livestock Farming (PLF), yet a critical barrier remains: high-performance Foundation Models (such as SAM 3) are too computationally intensive for edge deployment, while lightweight models (like YOLO) require prohibitive manual annotation efforts. This work proposes a fully automated knowledge distillation pipeline that leverages the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) to generate zero-shot pseudo-labels for training efficient YOLOv8 detectors. By treating SAM 3 as an offline auto-annotator, we eliminate the manual labeling bottleneck, producing models capable of real-time inference on resource-constrained hardware. We systematically evaluate this approach on the PigLife dataset, comparing SAM 3-supervised models against human-annotated baselines. Results demonstrate that a SAM 3-trained YOLOv8m achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 79.4% without human intervention, while reducing inference latency by approximately 200$\times$ compared to the teacher model. Furthermore, stratified analysis reveals that in low-occlusion scenarios, the automated pipeline achieves detection rates comparable to human benchmarks ($AP_{50} > 99\%$). These findings indicate that foundation models can serve as effective, zero-annotation-cost supervisors, enabling scalable edge computing solutions for smart agriculture.

</details>


### [174] [WBench: A Comprehensive Multi-turn Benchmark for Interactive Video World Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25874)
*Kaining Ying,Hengrui Hu,Siyu Ren,Jiamu Li,Fengjiao Chen,Ziwen Wang,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WBench是一个用于评估交互式世界模型的综合多轮基准，涵盖视频质量、设定遵循、交互遵循、一致性和物理合规性五个维度，包含289个测试用例和1058个交互轮次，对20个模型评估后发现没有模型在所有维度上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式世界模型发展迅速，但已有基准仅覆盖部分能力，缺乏统一的系统评估标准，因此需要构建一个全面的评估基准。

Method: 构建了WBench基准，包含289个测试用例和1058个交互轮次，每个用例指定世界设定和多轮交互序列，覆盖多种场景、风格、主体以及第一/第三人称视角，包含导航、主体动作、事件编辑和视角切换四种交互类型。导航统一了文本、6-DoF位姿和离散动作控制。使用22个自动子指标（结合专家视觉模型和大规模多模态模型）进行评估，所有指标经人工验证。

Result: 对20个最先进模型的评估显示，没有任何一个模型在所有维度上表现强劲，论文提供了各模型特征性优势、劣势和开放挑战的详细诊断分析。

Conclusion: WBench为交互式世界模型提供了系统化的多维度评估标准，揭示了当前模型的能力短板，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Interactive world models are advancing rapidly, yet existing benchmarks cover only part of the required competencies, leaving no unified standard for systematic evaluation. To fill this gap, we introduce WBench, a comprehensive multi-turn benchmark for interactive world model evaluation along five dimensions, namely video quality, setting adherence, interaction adherence, consistency, and physics compliance. WBench contains 289 test cases and 1,058 interaction turns, where each case specifies a world setting and a multi-turn interaction sequence, covering diverse scenes, styles, subjects, and both first- and third-person perspectives, together with four interaction types, including navigation, subject action, event editing, and perspective switching. For navigation, WBench unifies text, 6-DoF pose, and discrete-action control, enabling evaluation of models with different native input interfaces. Evaluation uses 22 automatic sub-metrics that combine specialist vision models with large multimodal models, and all metrics are validated against human judgments. Across 20 state-of-the-art models, we find that no single model performs strongly across all dimensions. We provide detailed diagnostic insights into the characteristic strengths, weaknesses, and open challenges of each model. Code and data are available at https://github.com/meituan-longcat/WBench.

</details>


### [175] [DyCoRM: Dynamic Criterion-Aware Reward Modeling for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25876)
*Jiaying Qian,Ziheng Jia,Qian Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Jiayi Guo,Junqi Zhang,Guangtao Zhai,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出DyCoRM，一个动态、标准感知的奖励模型，用于在文本到图像生成中根据用户特定、细粒度的动态标准评估图像质量。同时构建了DyCoDataset-20K数据集和DyCoBench-1K基准，并引入DyCoPick进行标准感知的图像选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着T2I生成质量的提升，用户需求转向更精准满足特定要求的图像。现有奖励模型仅依赖静态通用评价维度，无法捕捉任务相关和细粒度的用户标准。因此需要一种能感知动态、细粒度标准的奖励模型。

Method: 提出DyCoRM框架，包含三个部分：1) DyCoDataset-20K数据集，提供动态标准及标准级标注；2) DyCoBench-1K基准，系统评估动态标准下的奖励模型；3) DyCoPick方法，将标准感知奖励建模应用于T2I图像选择。

Result: 建立了首个面向动态细粒度评估的奖励建模框架，并验证了其在T2I生成中的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: DyCoRM首次将动态、细粒度的标准感知引入奖励建模，填补了现有奖励模型在用户特定需求评估方面的空白，为T2I生成中更精准的图像质量评估和选择提供了新范式。

Abstract: With the continued advancement of text-to-image (T2I) generation, producing high-quality images is becoming increasingly attainable; consequently, user demands are shifting toward images that better satisfy their specific requirements. As reward models play an increasingly important role in assessing whether generated images align with user preference, this trend introduces an important challenge for reward modeling: rather than relying solely on static and general evaluation dimensions, reward models should account for the task-relevant and fine-grained criteria through which users assess whether generated images meet their specific requirements. To address this challenge, we propose DyCoRM, a dynamic, criterion-aware reward model that grounds task-relevant criteria and performs criterion-aware preference comparison. To support this setting, we construct DyCoDataset-20K, which provides dynamic criteria together with criterion-level annotations, and further derive DyCoBench-1K, a benchmark for systematically evaluating reward models under dynamic criteria. We further introduce DyCoPick, which applies criterion-aware reward modeling to selecting T2I images. Our contributions establish the first reward modeling framework for dynamic and fine-grained evaluation and practical application in T2I generation.

</details>


### [176] [R5DGS: Semantic-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting with Rigid Body Constraints for Efficient Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25909)
*Denis Gridusov,Maxim Popov,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R5DGS是一个结合物理驱动4D高斯表示与身份编码的框架，通过CLIP-based对象查找表实现开放词汇的文本提示检索和渲染特定对象的高斯体，并利用刚体推理约束将预测和集成物理动力学对象质心，实现11 FPS的外推加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息高斯泼溅方法虽然能实现未来帧外推，但缺乏语义感知能力且计算开销大，无法高效地支持基于语义的对象级操作和渲染。

Method: 1) 在物理驱动的4D高斯表示中引入紧凑的身份编码向量，实现精确的高斯-对象关联；2) 构建离线CLIP-based对象查找表，支持开放词汇文本提示检索和渲染对象特定高斯体；3) 提出刚体推理约束，仅对对象质心进行物理动力学预测和集成，通过相对变换将运动传播到关联高斯体。

Result: 在外推过程中实现了11 FPS的速度提升，同时保持轨迹的合理性。

Conclusion: R5DGS在保持物理动力学预测能力的同时，大幅提升计算效率和语义感知能力，为动态3D场景的语义级操作和高效外推提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing and predicting dynamic 3D scenes from multi-view videos is a foundational task for robotics, AR/VR, and digital twins. Recent physics-informed Gaussian Splatting methods achieve impressive future frame extrapolation but lack semantic awareness and suffer from large computational overhead. We introduce $\textbf{R5DGS}$, a framework that augments a physics-driven 4D Gaussian representation with compact Identity Encoding vectors, enabling precise Gaussian-to-object association. By constructing an offline CLIP-based object lookup table, we support open-vocabulary text prompting to retrieve and render object-specific Gaussians across arbitrary timestamps and viewpoints. Furthermore, we propose a rigid-body inference constraint that predicts and integrates physical dynamics exclusively for object centroids, propagating motion to associated Gaussians via relative transformations. This optimization yields a 11 FPS speedup during extrapolation without compromising trajectories plausibility.

</details>


### [177] [Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting for Adversarial Robustness of Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25922)
*Xiao Liu,Jiaxiang Liu,Boci Peng,Boren Hu,Yusong Wang,Xiwen Chen,Prayag Tiwari,Liming Zhang,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出闭环双向提示（Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting），通过动态反馈循环和语义锚点实现跨模态语义对齐恢复，在11个数据集上取得了最先进的鲁棒性和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法多为单向或静态的，未能利用双向跨模态互补性和实例级自适应保护，难以应对对抗性扰动对跨模态语义对齐的破坏。

Method: 提出闭环双向提示方法，引入语义锚点作为稳定先验约束循环更新，通过锚点引导的引导机制：文本语义去噪视觉表示，精炼后的视觉表示再实现实例自适应提示更新，形成矫正的鲁棒共识。

Result: 在11个数据集上实现最先进的鲁棒性，表现出强的基础到新类泛化能力，并在计算成本与准确性之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 闭环双向提示通过双向跨模态互补和实例级自适应保护，有效克服了单向和静态防御的局限性，显著提升了视觉语言模型在对抗环境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision Language Models adapt well to downstream tasks but are highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations that disrupt cross-modal semantic alignment. Existing defenses are largely unidirectional or structural, failing to exploit bidirectional cross-modal complementarity and instance-wise adaptive protection. To overcome the limitations of unidirectional and static defenses in adversarial settings, we propose Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting, casting robust adaptation as cross-modal agreement recovery via a dynamic feedback loop on frozen encoders. A Semantic Anchor is introduced as a stable prior to constrain cyclic updates and mitigate perturbation-induced feature corruption. Through anchor-based bootstrapping, textual semantics denoise visual representations, while the refined visuals enable instance-adaptive prompt updating, yielding a rectified and robust consensus. Extensive evaluations across 11 datasets validate state-of-the-art robustness and strong base-to-new generalization, while maintaining a favorable trade-off between computational cost and accuracy.

</details>


### [178] [Where Concept Erasure Should Occur: Concept-Layer Alignment in Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25941)
*Yiwei Xie,Ping Liu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到视频扩散Transformer中概念信息在模型深度上分布不均匀，存在概念-层拓扑对齐瓶颈。基于此提出CLEAR框架，通过可分离性驱动的优化进行概念擦除，在保持生成质量的同时实现更精确的概念抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法未能考虑概念信息在模型深度上的不均匀编码特性，导致在深度无关或启发式选择的层上进行擦除效果受限。

Method: 提出CLEAR框架：将层选择建模为概念-非目标可分离性的优化问题，引入可分离性感知目标函数，优先选择概念-非目标分离更强的层进行擦除。

Result: 在大规模文本到视频扩散模型上的实验表明，强制概念-层对齐能够在保持整体生成质量的同时实现更精确的概念抑制。

Conclusion: 概念擦除应被重新定义为寻找概念-非目标自然分离的表征深度的问题，而CLEAR通过显式强制概念-层对齐有效解决了这一挑战。

Abstract: Text-to-video diffusion transformers encode semantic information unevenly across model depth, which constrains effective concept erasure. We identify a representational bottleneck, termed concept-layer topological alignment, under which target concepts exhibit higher separability at certain representational depths. Outside these depths, concept and non-target signals remain strongly entangled, limiting the effectiveness of depth-specific erasure. This observation reframes concept erasure as the problem of identifying representational depths where concept-non-target separation naturally emerges. Motivated by this structural constraint, we introduce CLEAR, a separability-driven optimization framework for concept erasure that explicitly enforces concept-layer alignment. CLEAR operationalizes this principle by formulating layer selection as an optimization problem over concept-non-target separability, rather than relying on layer-agnostic or heuristic choices. To enable this, we introduce a separability-aware objective that favors layers exhibiting stronger concept-non-target separation. Experiments on large-scale text-to-video diffusion models demonstrate that enforcing concept--layer alignment leads to more precise concept suppression while preserving overall generative quality.

</details>


### [179] [LRDDv3: High-Resolution Long-Range Drone Detection Dataset with Range Information and Thermal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25942)
*Knut Peterson,Zaid Mayers,Azmain Yousuf,Priontu Chowdhury,Asher Zaczepinski,Solmaz Arezoomandan,Reihaneh Maarefdoust,David Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个高分辨率远程无人机检测数据集LRDDv3，包含102,532张RGB图像（4K分辨率）和29,630张配对IR图像，覆盖多种光照、场景和背景，旨在提升无人机远程检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机在各空域的广泛应用，确保有人/无人航空器能在远距离可靠检测无人机以保障共享空域安全至关重要，但现有无人机检测数据集在高质量远程数据方面仍存在不足。

Method: 收集了128个视频片段，采样为5 FPS的102,532张4K RGB图像和29,630张配对的640x512 IR图像，采集跨越8个月共17天，涵盖多种光照条件、飞行地点和背景元素，并提供了详细的无人机距离信息。

Result: 构建了首个同时提供4K RGB图像和配对640x512 IR图像的无人机检测数据集，包含全面距离标注，为远程无人机检测提供了重要数据基础。

Conclusion: 该高分辨率远程无人机数据集填补了现有数据集的空白，有望推动远程无人机检测技术的显著进步，提升共享空域安全。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have quickly become common in various airspaces, representing a wide range of applications from recreation flying to commercial photography and package delivery. With the increasing prevalence of UAVs, it becomes critical that both manned and unmanned aircraft can detect UAVs and other flying objects from long range to effectively track movement and ensure safe operation in shared spaces. While several datasets have been introduced for drone detection, the need for expanded high-quality data persists, especially in the area of high-resolution long-range drone data.
